with
\vartheta_{\ell}=\frac{r_{\ell}}{\sqrt{1-r_{\ell}^{2}}}<1
, the left-hand side of (
39
) is lower bounded by

\displaystyle\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]+\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\kappa_{R}^{2}}{4}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}-\underbrace{\sup_{u\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\pi(X_{i})\left(X_{i}^{T}u\right)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[\pi(X_{i})\left(X_{i}^{T}u\right)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}_{\textbf{Term I}}

\displaystyle\quad-\underbrace{\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\end{subarray}}\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)\left[X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]\right|\cdot r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}}_{\textbf{Term II}}.

We now derive bounds on the remaining two terms. Recall our growth condition
\sqrt{\frac{s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}}<1
. Using the same arguments in Lemma
E.10
, we know that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

Term I

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{u\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\pi(X_{i})\left(X_{i}^{T}u\right)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[\pi(X_{i})\left(X_{i}^{T}u\right)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\pi(X_{i})\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[\pi(X_{i})\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}.

In addition, by Lemma
E.10
again, we know that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\textbf{Term II}\lesssim r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left[\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+1\right].

Now, combining (
39
) and (
40
) with the above two bounds yields that there exists an absolute constant
A_{0}>0
such that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\left[\frac{\kappa_{R}^{2}}{4}-A_{0}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}\right]\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-A_{0}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+1\right]\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\cdot r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6\gamma}{5n}\sqrt{s_{\ell}(x)}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}.

By our growth condition
\sqrt{\frac{s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}}<\min\left\{\frac{\kappa_{R}^{2}}{A_{1}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}},1\right\}
with
A_{1}=8A_{0}
, it follows that

\frac{\kappa_{R}^{2}}{4}-A_{0}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}\geq\frac{\kappa_{R}^{2}}{8},

and therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa_{R}^{2}}{8}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6\gamma}{5n}\sqrt{s_{\ell}(x)}+2A_{0}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+1\right]\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\cdot r_{\ell}\cdot\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\gamma}{n}\sqrt{s_{\ell}(x)}+\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\cdot r_{\ell}\cdot\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}

Dividing both sides of the above inequality by
\frac{\kappa_{R}^{2}}{8}
gives us that

\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\lesssim\frac{\gamma\sqrt{s_{\ell}(x)}}{n\cdot\kappa_{R}^{2}}+\frac{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\cdot r_{\ell}.

Finally, the bound on
\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}
follows from substituting
\frac{\gamma}{n}\equiv A_{2}\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}}}{\kappa_{R}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}}

into the upper bound of
\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}
in (a).
∎

With the above lemma, we can now formally prove the consistency of the dual solution
\widehat{\ell}(x)
(
Theorem 4
). Notice that the major difference between the proof of Lemma
E.1
and
Theorem 4
is that the propensity scores now are estimated in
Theorem 4
.

Theorem 4

(Consistency of the dual solution
\widehat{\ell}(x)
)
.

Let
\delta\in(0,1)
,
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
be a fixed covariate vector, and
A_{1},A_{2},A_{3}>0
be some large absolute constants. Suppose that Assumptions
1
,
2
,
4
,
5
, and
6
hold
as well as

\sqrt{\frac{s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}}+r_{\pi}<\min\left\{\frac{\kappa_{R}^{2}}{A_{1}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}},1\right\}\quad\text{ and }\quad d\geq s_{\ell}(x)+2.

We denote
r_{\gamma}\equiv r_{\gamma}(n,\delta)=\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}}}{\kappa_{R}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}}+\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\cdot r_{\pi}
. If
\frac{\gamma}{n}=A_{2}\cdot r_{\gamma}
and

r_{\ell}\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{2},\,\left[\left(\frac{A_{2}^{2}\,\kappa_{R}^{6}\,\phi_{1}}{A_{3}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}+\left(\frac{A_{2}^{2}\phi_{1}\kappa_{R}^{4}}{A_{3}^{2}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\sqrt{r_{\pi}}\right]\right\},

then with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{3}}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}s_{\ell}(x)\log(d/\delta)}{n}}+\frac{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}{\kappa_{R}}\cdot r_{\ell}+\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}s_{\ell}(x)}}{\kappa_{R}}\cdot r_{\pi}\right].

Proof of
Theorem 4
.

Since
\widehat{\ell}(x)
minimizes the objective function of the dual problem (
8
), we know that

\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left[X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\ell}(x)\right]^{2}+x^{T}\widehat{\ell}(x)+\frac{\gamma}{n}\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}\leq\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left[X_{i}^{T}\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{2}+x^{T}\widetilde{\ell}(x)+\frac{\gamma}{n}\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}.

Some rearrangements (or using Taylor’s expansion) give us that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]+\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]\\
&\leq\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)+x\right]^{T}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\widehat{\ell}(x)\right]+\frac{\gamma}{n}\left[\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}-\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}\right].\end{split}

(41)

We now reduce the above inequality to (
39
) in Lemma
E.1
so that we can apply parts of the results in Lemma
E.1
. Some algebra show that the above inequality is equivalent to

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\underbrace{\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]+\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]}_{\textbf{Term I}}\\
&\quad+\underbrace{\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right)X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]}_{\textbf{Term II}}\\
&\quad+\underbrace{\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right)X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]}_{\textbf{Term III}}\\
&\leq\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)+x\right]^{T}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\widehat{\ell}(x)\right]+\frac{\gamma}{n}\left[\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}-\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}\right].\end{split}

(42)

We first obtain an upper bound on the right-hand side of the above inequality via the same arguments as in the proof of Lemma
E.1
. We write
\widehat{\Delta}(x)=\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)
. By Hölder’s inequality and the (reverse) triangle’s inequality together with the sparsity of
\widetilde{\ell}(x)
under Assumption
6
, the right-hand side of (
41
) is upper bounded by

\displaystyle\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)+x\right]^{T}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\widehat{\ell}(x)\right]+\frac{\gamma}{n}\left[\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}-\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)+x\right|\right|_{\infty}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\gamma}{n}\left[\left|\left|\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)}\right|\right|_{1}-\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)}+\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)}\right|\right|_{1}-\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\right|\right|_{1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\gamma}{5n}\left[\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)}\right|\right|_{1}+\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\right|\right|_{1}\right]+\frac{\gamma}{n}\left[\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)}\right|\right|_{1}-\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\right|\right|_{1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6\gamma}{5n}\sqrt{s_{\ell}(x)}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

where
\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)}\right|\right|_{1}=\sum_{k\in S_{\ell}(x)}\left|\widehat{\Delta}_{k}(x)\right|
.

Next, we derive lower bounds for the terms on the left-hand side of (
42
). We know from Lemma
E.9
by
a_{0}=5
that

\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\in\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),3\right)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}\left(0,\,\frac{13r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{2\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{\nu}{\gamma}\right)\equiv\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),3\right)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1}),

where
B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r)=\left\{v\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:\left|\left|v\right|\right|_{1}\leq r\right\}
and
r_{1}\equiv\frac{13r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{2\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{\nu}{\gamma}
.

Now, by Lemma
E.11
, there exist absolute constants
A_{2},A_{2}^{\prime}>0
such that

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)+2x\right|\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq A_{2}^{\prime}\cdot\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}}}{\kappa_{R}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}}+\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+A_{2}^{\prime}\cdot\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\left[1+\sqrt{\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}}+\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}\right]r_{\pi}(n,\delta)

\displaystyle\lesssim A_{2}\cdot\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}}}{\kappa_{R}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}}+A_{2}\cdot\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\cdot r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\equiv\frac{\gamma}{n},

where we apply our growth condition
\sqrt{\frac{s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}}<1
. Similarly, we obtain from Lemma
E.11
that there exist absolute constants
A_{3},A_{3}^{\prime}>0
such that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}\left(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell}\right)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left[X_{i}^{T}v\right]^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2A_{3}^{\prime}}{13}\cdot\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left[1+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log\left(d/s_{\ell}(x)\right)}{n}}\right]\left[1+r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2A_{3}}{13}\cdot\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\equiv\frac{\nu}{n},

where we apply our growth condition
\sqrt{\frac{s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}}+r_{\pi}<1
again. Hence, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\frac{\kappa_{R}^{2}}{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}\sqrt{\frac{2\gamma}{13\nu}}\geq\left(\frac{A_{2}^{2}\,\kappa_{R}^{6}\,\phi_{1}}{A_{3}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}+\left(\frac{A_{2}^{2}\phi_{1}\kappa_{R}^{4}}{A_{3}^{2}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\sqrt{r_{\pi}}>r_{\ell},

where the last inequality follows from our condition. In other words, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have that
r_{1}=\frac{13r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{2\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{\nu}{\gamma}<1
, and

\left[\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),3\right)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\right]\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}=\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),3\right)\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}.

\bullet

Term I:
This term coincides with the left-hand side of (
39
) in the proof of Lemma
E.1
. Thus, we conclude that there exists absolute constant
A,A_{0}>0
such that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

Term I

\displaystyle\geq\left[\frac{\kappa_{R}^{2}}{4}-A\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}\right]\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-A\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+1\right]\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\cdot r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\left[\frac{\kappa_{R}^{2}}{4}-A_{0}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}\right]\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}-A_{0}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\cdot r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2},

where we use our growth condition
\sqrt{\frac{s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}}<1
to obtain the second inequality.

\bullet

Term II:
By Lemmas
E.7
and
E.10
, we have that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\left|\textbf{Term II}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\widehat{\Delta}(x)\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell},3)\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right|\left(X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\widehat{\Delta}(x)\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell},3)\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\frac{r_{\pi}(n,\delta)}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\cdot r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+1\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\cdot r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2},

where we again use our growth condition
\sqrt{\frac{s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}}<1
.

\bullet

Term III:
By Assumption
6
, Lemmas
E.8
and
E.10
with
\vartheta_{\ell}=\frac{r_{\ell}}{\sqrt{1-r_{\ell}^{2}}}<1
and
r_{\ell}\leq\frac{1}{2}
,

\displaystyle\left|\textbf{Term III}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell},3)\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\end{subarray}}\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right|\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right||X_{i}^{T}v|\right]r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell},3)\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\end{subarray}}\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right||X_{i}^{T}v|\right]r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\cdot r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\cdot\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}}+1\right]

Under the growth condition
\sqrt{\frac{s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}}<1
, we have that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\left|\textbf{Term III}\right|\lesssim\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\cdot r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\cdot r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}.

Combining (
42
) with the above three bounds yields that there exists an absolute constant
A_{1}>0
such that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\left[\frac{\kappa_{R}^{2}}{4}-\frac{A_{1}}{16}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}-\frac{A_{1}}{16}\cdot\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\cdot r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\right]\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-A_{1}\left[1+r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\right]\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6\gamma}{5n}\sqrt{s_{\ell}(x)}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}.

By our growth condition
\sqrt{\frac{s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}}+r_{\pi}<\min\left\{\frac{\kappa_{R}^{2}}{A_{1}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}},1\right\}
, we conclude by dividing both sides of the inequality by
\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}
and
\frac{\kappa_{R}^{2}}{8}
that

\displaystyle\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{48\gamma}{5\kappa_{R}^{2}n}\sqrt{s_{\ell}(x)}+\frac{8A_{1}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\left[1+r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\right]\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\gamma\sqrt{s_{\ell}(x)}}{n\cdot\kappa_{R}^{2}}+\frac{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot r_{\ell}.

Finally, the bound for
\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}
follows by substituting the asymptotic rate of
\frac{\gamma}{n}
into the upper bound of
\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}
.
∎

E.5
Proofs of Lemma
5
and Lemma
6

Lemma 5

(Sufficient conditions for strong duality)
.

Let
\delta\in(0,1)
. Under the assumptions of
Theorem 4
, there exists
\gamma>0
such that
\frac{\gamma}{n}\asymp r_{\gamma}\equiv r_{\gamma}(n,\delta)
and strong duality as well as the relation (
9
) in Proposition
1
hold with probability at least
1-\delta
.

Proof of Lemma
5
.

Given that the primal problem (
4
) is convex, it suffices to verify that Slater’s condition holds with probability at least
1-\delta
. That is, it suffices to show that the interior of the constraint set defined as

\left\{\bm{w}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:\left|\left|x-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\cdot\widehat{\pi}_{i}\cdot X_{i}\right|\right|_{\infty}<\frac{\gamma}{n}\right\}

is nonempty for some
\gamma>0
satisfying
\frac{\gamma}{n}\asymp r_{\gamma}
. To this end, we define

\displaystyle\bm{w}^{*}(x)=\left(w_{1}^{*}(x),...,w_{n}^{*}(x)\right)^{T}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{X}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}X_{1}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x,...,\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}X_{n}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x\right)^{T}

with
\bm{X}=(X_{1},...,X_{n})^{T}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times d}
. The inverse
\left[\mathrm{E}(RXX^{T})\right]^{-1}=\left[\mathrm{E}\left(\pi(X)XX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}
is valid under Assumption
4
. Then,

\displaystyle\left|\left|x-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{*}(x)\cdot\widehat{\pi}_{i}\cdot X_{i}\right|\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left|\left|x-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{*}(x)\cdot\pi_{i}\cdot X_{i}\right|\right|_{\infty}}_{\textbf{Term I}}+\underbrace{\left|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{*}(x)\left(\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right)X_{i}\right|\right|_{\infty}}_{\textbf{Term II}}.

\bullet

Term I:
We know that
\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{*}(x)\cdot\pi_{i}\cdot X_{i}\right]=x
. Thus, by Assumption
2
and Lemma 2.7.7 in
Vershynin 2018
, all the random variables

x_{1}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(e_{1}^{T}X_{i}\right)\pi_{i}\cdot w_{i}^{*}(x)\,,\,...\,,\,x_{d}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(e_{d}^{T}X_{i}\right)\pi_{i}\cdot w_{i}^{*}(x)

have zero means and sub-exponential tails (but neither identically distributed nor independent), where
e_{1},...,e_{d}
are the standard basis vectors in
\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Moreover, we can write

x_{k}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(e_{k}^{T}X_{i}\right)\pi_{i}\cdot w_{i}^{*}(x)=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(x_{k}-\pi(X_{i})X_{ik}X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}(X_{1}R_{1}X_{1}^{T})\right]^{-1}x\right)

for all
k=1,...,d
. By Assumption
2
and Lemma
E.13
, we know that

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq k\leq d}\left|\left|x_{k}-\pi(X_{i})X_{ik}X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}(X_{1}R_{1}X_{1}^{T})\right]^{-1}x\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq k\leq d}\left(\left|\left|\pi(X_{i})X_{ik}X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}(X_{1}R_{1}X_{1}^{T})\right]^{-1}x\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}+\frac{1}{\log 2}\left|\mathrm{E}\left\{\pi(X_{i})X_{ik}X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}(X_{1}R_{1}X_{1}^{T})\right]^{-1}x\right\}\right|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\frac{1}{\log 2}\right)\max_{1\leq k\leq d}\left|\left|\pi(X_{i})X_{ik}X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}(X_{1}R_{1}X_{1}^{T})\right]^{-1}x\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{1\leq k\leq d}\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\left|X_{ik}\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\left|\left|\pi(X_{i})X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}(X_{1}R_{1}X_{1}^{T})\right]^{-1}x\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}}}{\kappa_{R}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}

for all
1\leq i\leq n
. Therefore, by the union bound and Bernstein’s inequality (Corollary 2.8.3 in
Vershynin 2018
), we know that there exists an absolute constant
A_{0}>0
such that for all
t>0
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\left(\left|\left|x-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{*}(x)\cdot\pi_{i}\cdot X_{i}\right|\right|_{\infty}>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq d\cdot\max_{1\leq k\leq d}\mathrm{P}\left\{\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(x_{k}-\pi(X_{i})X_{ik}X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}(X_{1}R_{1}X_{1}^{T})\right]^{-1}x\right)\right|>t\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 2d\cdot\exp\left(-nA_{0}\min\left\{\frac{t\kappa_{R}}{\sqrt{\phi_{1}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}},\,\frac{t^{2}\kappa_{R}^{2}}{\phi_{1}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}\right\}\right).

In other words, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\textbf{Term I}=\left|\left|x-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{X}^{T}\bm{\Pi}w^{*}(x)\right|\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(2d/\delta)}{n}}+\frac{\log(2d/\delta)}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}},

where we apply our growth condition
\sqrt{\frac{s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}}<1
in the last asymptotic inequality.

\bullet

Term II:
We know from the proof of Lemma
E.12
(specifically (
63
)) that

Term II

\displaystyle=\left|\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}^{*}(x)\left(\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right)X_{i}\right|\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\left|\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right)X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x\right|\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\left|\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right)X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\cdot r_{\pi}(n,\delta),

where we again apply our growth condition
\sqrt{\frac{s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}}<1
in the last asymptotic inequality.

In summary, we conclude that there exists an absolute constant
A_{0}>0
such that with probability at least
1-\delta
, the constraint set is nonempty for all
\gamma>0
satisfying

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma}{n}

\displaystyle\gtrsim A_{0}\left[\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}}+\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}\cdot\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\cdot r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\right]

\displaystyle\equiv A_{0}\cdot r_{\gamma}(n,\delta)

that is defined in
Theorem 4
. In particular, there exists
\gamma>0
such that
\frac{\gamma}{n}\asymp r_{\gamma}(n,\delta)
and Slater’s condition holds.
∎

Lemma 6

(Negligible conditional variance term)
.

Suppose that Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
hold
as well as

\left[s_{\beta}\log(d/s_{\beta})+s_{\ell}(x)\log(d/s_{\ell}(x))\right]^{2}=O\left(\sqrt{n}\right),\quad\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\sqrt{\phi_{2s_{\ell}(x)}\phi_{2s_{\beta}}}=O(1),\quad\text{ and }\quad r_{\ell}=O(1).

If
\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}=o_{P}(1)
and
\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}=o_{P}(1)
, then the “Conditional variance II” term in Proposition
2
is asymptotically negligible in the sense that

\displaystyle\left(\beta_{0}-\widehat{\beta}\right)^{T}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{w}_{i}(x)^{2}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left(1-\widehat{\pi}_{i}\right)X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\left(\beta_{0}-\widehat{\beta}\right)=o_{P}(1).

Proof of Lemma
6
.

By Lemma
5
, strong duality holds with probability at least
1-\delta
under our assumptions. Moreover, by the proofs of
Theorem 3
and
Theorem 4
, we know that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\beta},3),\quad\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\in\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),3\right)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}\left(0,\,\frac{13r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{2\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{\nu}{\gamma}\right)\equiv\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),3\right)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1}),

where
r_{1}\equiv\frac{13r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{2\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{\nu}{\gamma}<1
under the condition in
Theorem 4
. In other words, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\left[\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),3\right)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\right]\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}=\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),3\right)\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}.

Thus, we derive from (
9
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left(\beta_{0}-\widehat{\beta}\right)^{T}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{w}_{i}(x)^{2}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left(1-\widehat{\pi}_{i}\right)X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\left(\beta_{0}-\widehat{\beta}\right)\\
&=\left(\beta_{0}-\widehat{\beta}\right)^{T}\left[\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left(1-\widehat{\pi}_{i}\right)\left[X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\ell}(x)\right]^{2}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\left(\beta_{0}-\widehat{\beta}\right).\end{split}

(43)

Notice that we do not assume the estimated propensity scores
\widehat{\pi}_{i},i=1,...,n
to be within
[0,1]
. In order to eliminate the factor
\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left(1-\widehat{\pi}_{i}\right)
in each summand, we observe the identity that
a(1-a)-b(1-b)=(1-2b)(a-b)-(a-b)^{2}
for all
a,b\in\mathbb{R}
. Hence, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have that

\left|\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left(1-\widehat{\pi}_{i}\right)-\pi_{i}(1-\pi_{i})\right|\leq\left|\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right|+\left|\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right|^{2}\lesssim r_{\pi}

for all
i=1,...,n
, where the last asymptotic inequality follows from Assumption
5
. Moreover, since
\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\pi_{i}(1-\pi_{i})\right|\leq\frac{1}{4}
, we can upper bound (
43
) (up to an absolute constant) with probability at least
1-\delta
by

\left(\beta_{0}-\widehat{\beta}\right)^{T}\left[\frac{(1+r_{\pi})}{16n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\ell}(x)\right]^{2}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\left(\beta_{0}-\widehat{\beta}\right).

We proceed to further upper bound this quantity as:

\displaystyle\left(\beta_{0}-\widehat{\beta}\right)^{T}\left[\frac{(1+r_{\pi})}{16n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\ell}(x)\right]^{2}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\left(\beta_{0}-\widehat{\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\leq(1+r_{\pi})\left(\beta_{0}-\widehat{\beta}\right)^{T}\left[\frac{1}{16n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right)\right]^{2}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}+\frac{1}{16n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[X_{i}^{T}\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{2}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\left(\beta_{0}-\widehat{\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\lesssim(1+r_{\pi})\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\Biggl[\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),3\right)\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\beta},3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{T}u\right)^{2}\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\\
&\hskip 99.58464pt+\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1},\left|\left|u\right|\right|_{0}\leq s_{\ell}(x)\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\beta},3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\end{subarray}}\frac{\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{T}u\right)^{2}\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\Biggr],\end{split}

(44)

where
\widehat{\Delta}(x)=\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)
.

Now, by Lemma
E.4
, there exist discrete sets
M_{d,\ell},M_{d,\beta}\subset B_{d}(0,1)
with cardinality
|M_{d,\ell}|\leq\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{5ed}{s_{\ell}(x)}\right)^{s_{\ell}(x)}
and
|M_{d,\beta}|\leq\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{5ed}{s_{\beta}}\right)^{s_{\beta}}
such that
\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\subset 10\cdot\mathrm{conv}(M_{d,\ell})
,
\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\beta},3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\subset 10\cdot\mathrm{conv}(M_{d,\beta})
and
\left|\left|u\right|\right|_{0}\leq s_{\ell}(x),\left|\left|v\right|\right|_{0}\leq s_{\beta}
for all
u\in M_{d,\ell},v\in M_{d,\beta}
. Moreover, we know from Lemma
E.5
that the supremum of a biconvex function on
\mathrm{conv}(M_{d,\ell})
or
\mathrm{conv}(M_{d,\beta})
is attained at its vertices, which are contained in
M_{d,\ell}
or
M_{d,\beta}
. Hence, we can upper bound the above display as:

\displaystyle(1+r_{\pi})\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\Bigg[\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in M_{d,\ell}\\
v\in M_{d,\beta}\end{subarray}}\frac{\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{T}u\right)^{2}\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 99.58464pt+\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in M_{d,\ell}\\
v\in M_{d,\beta}\end{subarray}}\frac{\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{T}u\right)^{2}\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq(1+r_{\pi})\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}+(1+r_{\ell})^{2}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in M_{d,\ell}\\
v\in M_{d,\beta}\end{subarray}}\mathrm{E}\left[(X^{T}u)^{2}(X^{T}v)^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left|\left|\mathbb{G}_{n}\right|\right|_{\mathcal{F}}\right],

where

\mathcal{F}=\left\{f_{u,v}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}:f_{u,v}(X)=\left(X^{T}u\right)^{2}\left(X^{T}v\right)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[\left(X^{T}u\right)^{2}\left(X^{T}v\right)^{2}\right],\,u\in M_{d,\ell},v\in M_{d,\beta}\right\}

and we utilize Assumption
6
to obtain that

\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\leq\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}+\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\leq(1+r_{\ell})\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

in the last inequality. Now, by Assumption
2
, we know from Cauchy-Schwarz inequality that

\displaystyle\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in M_{d,\ell}\\
v\in M_{d,\beta}\end{subarray}}\mathrm{E}\left[(X^{T}u)^{2}(X^{T}v)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{u\in M_{d,\ell}}\sqrt{\mathrm{E}\left[(X^{T}u)^{4}\right]}\sup_{v\in M_{d,\beta}}\sqrt{\mathrm{E}\left[(X^{T}v)^{4}\right]}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{u\in M_{d,\ell}}\left|\left|X^{T}u\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}\sup_{v\in M_{d,\beta}}\left|\left|X^{T}v\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\phi_{s_{\beta}}.

Furthermore, for any
f_{u_{1},v_{1}},f_{u_{2},v_{2}}\in\mathcal{F}
and
\alpha=\frac{1}{2}
, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\left|f_{u_{1},v_{1}}-f_{u_{2},v_{2}}\right|\right|_{\mathrm{P},\psi_{\alpha}}

\displaystyle=\left|\left|\left(X^{T}u_{1}\right)^{2}\left(X^{T}v_{1}\right)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[\left(X^{T}u_{1}\right)^{2}\left(X^{T}v_{1}\right)^{2}\right]-\left(X^{T}u_{2}\right)^{2}\left(X^{T}v_{2}\right)^{2}+\mathrm{E}\left[\left(X^{T}u_{2}\right)^{2}\left(X^{T}v_{2}\right)^{2}\right]\right|\right|_{\psi_{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(v)}}}{{\lesssim}}\left|\left|\left(X^{T}u_{1}\right)^{2}\left(X^{T}v_{1}\right)^{2}-\left(X^{T}u_{2}\right)^{2}\left(X^{T}v_{2}\right)^{2}\right|\right|_{\psi_{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\left|\left[\left(X^{T}u_{1}\right)^{2}-\left(X^{T}u_{2}\right)^{2}\right]\left(X^{T}v_{1}\right)^{2}\right|\right|_{\psi_{\alpha}}+\left|\left|\left(X^{T}u_{2}\right)^{2}\left[\left(X^{T}v_{1}\right)^{2}-\left(X^{T}v_{2}\right)^{2}\right]\right|\right|_{\psi_{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(vi)}}}{{\leq}}\left|\left|X^{T}(u_{1}+u_{2})\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\left|\left|X^{T}(u_{1}-u_{2})\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\left|\left|X^{T}v_{1}\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}+\left|\left|X^{T}u_{2}\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}\left|\left|X^{T}(v_{1}+v_{2})\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\left|\left|X^{T}(v_{1}-v_{2})\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(vii)}}}{{\lesssim}}\phi_{2s_{\ell}(x)}\phi_{s_{\beta}}\left|\left|u_{1}-u_{2}\right|\right|_{2}+\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\phi_{2s_{\beta}}\left|\left|v_{1}-v_{2}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\phi_{2s_{\ell}(x)}\phi_{2s_{\beta}}\sqrt{\left|\left|u_{1}-u_{2}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}+\left|\left|v_{1}-v_{2}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}},

where the asymptotic inequality (v) follows from the fact that

\left|\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\left(X^{T}u_{1}\right)^{2}\left(X^{T}v_{1}\right)^{2}-\left(X^{T}u_{2}\right)^{2}\left(X^{T}v_{2}\right)^{2}\right]\right|\right|_{\psi_{\alpha}}\lesssim\left|\left|\left(X^{T}u_{1}\right)^{2}\left(X^{T}v_{1}\right)^{2}-\left(X^{T}u_{2}\right)^{2}\left(X^{T}v_{2}\right)^{2}\right|\right|_{\psi_{\alpha}}

because for any random variable
X
with
\left|\left|X\right|\right|_{\psi_{\alpha}}<\infty
, we know that
\mathrm{P}\left(|X|>t\right)\leq 2e^{-\frac{t^{\alpha}}{\left|\left|X\right|\right|_{\psi_{\alpha}}^{\alpha}}}
and

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}|X|^{p}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{P}\left(|X|>t\right)pt^{p-1}dt

\displaystyle\leq 2\int_{0}^{\infty}pt^{p-1}e^{-\frac{t^{\alpha}}{\left|\left|X\right|\right|_{\psi_{\alpha}}^{\alpha}}}dt

\displaystyle=\frac{2p}{\alpha}\left|\left|X\right|\right|_{\psi_{\alpha}}^{p}\int_{0}^{\infty}u^{\frac{p}{\alpha}-1}e^{-u}du\quad\text{ as we take }u=\frac{t^{\alpha}}{\left|\left|X\right|\right|_{\psi_{\alpha}}^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2p}{\alpha}\cdot\Gamma\left(\frac{p}{\alpha}\right)\cdot\left|\left|X\right|\right|_{\psi_{\alpha}}^{p},

where
\Gamma(\cdot)
is the Gamma function. In addition, the inequality (vi) is due to Lemma
E.13
and (vii) uses the fact that
\left|\left|u_{1}\pm u_{2}\right|\right|_{0}\leq 2s_{\ell}(x)
and
\left|\left|v_{1}\pm v_{2}\right|\right|_{0}\leq 2s_{\beta}
for any
u_{1},u_{2}\in M_{d,\ell}
and
v_{1},v_{2}\in M_{d,\beta}
. In particular,
\left|\left|f_{u,v}-f_{0,0}\right|\right|_{\mathrm{P},\psi_{\alpha}}=\left|\left|f_{u,v}\right|\right|_{\mathrm{P},\psi_{\alpha}}
also satisfies the above bound. The diameter of
\mathcal{F}
with respect to the norm

\varrho\left((u_{1},v_{1}),(u_{2},v_{2})\right):=\sqrt{\left|\left|u_{1}-u_{2}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}+\left|\left|v_{1}-v_{2}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}

is at most 2. Moreover, by Lemma
E.4
, the covering number satisfies that for any
\varepsilon>0
,

N\left(\varepsilon,M_{d,\ell}\times M_{d,\beta},\varrho\right)\leq|M_{d,\ell}|\times|M_{d,\beta}|\leq\frac{9}{4}\left(\frac{5ed}{s_{\ell}(x)}\right)^{s_{\ell}(x)}\left(\frac{5ed}{s_{\beta}}\right)^{s_{\beta}}.

Thus, by Lemma
E.14
and Remark
E.4
, we obtain that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left|\left|\mathbb{G}_{n}\right|\right|_{\mathcal{F}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left|\left|\mathbb{G}_{n}\right|\right|_{\mathcal{F}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\phi_{2s_{\ell}(x)}\phi_{2s_{\beta}}}{\sqrt{n}}\Biggl[\int_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\log N\left(\varepsilon,M_{d,\ell}\times M_{d,\beta},\varrho\right)}d\varepsilon

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\int_{0}^{2}\left[\log N\left(\varepsilon,M_{d,\ell}\times M_{d,\beta},\varrho\right)\right]^{2}d\varepsilon+2\left(\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}+\frac{\left[\log(1/\delta)\right]^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\Biggr]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\phi_{2s_{\ell}(x)}\phi_{2s_{\beta}}}{\sqrt{n}}\Biggl[\sqrt{s_{\ell}(x)\log\left(\frac{ed}{s_{\ell}(x)}\right)+s_{\beta}\log\left(\frac{ed}{s_{\beta}}\right)}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(s_{\ell}(x)\log\left(\frac{ed}{s_{\ell}(x)}\right)+s_{\beta}\log\left(\frac{ed}{s_{\beta}}\right)\right)^{2}+\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}+\frac{\left[\log(1/\delta)\right]^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\Biggr]

\displaystyle\lesssim\phi_{2s_{\ell}(x)}\phi_{2s_{\beta}}\left[\sqrt{\frac{s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell(x)})}{n}+\frac{s_{\beta}\log(ed/s_{\beta})}{n}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.+\left(\frac{s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell(x)})}{\sqrt{n}}+\frac{s_{\beta}\log(ed/s_{\beta})}{\sqrt{n}}\right)^{2}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}}+\left(\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}}\right)^{2}\right].

Set
\delta=\frac{1}{\max\{d,n\}}
. By our growth conditions
\left[s_{\beta}\log(d/s_{\beta})+s_{\ell}(x)\log(d/s_{\ell}(x))\right]^{2}=O\left(\sqrt{n}\right)
and
\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\sqrt{\phi_{2s_{\ell}(x)}\phi_{2s_{\beta}}}=O(1)
, we conclude that
\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left|\left|\mathbb{G}_{n}\right|\right|_{\mathcal{F}}=O_{P}(1)
.

Finally, combining our above results with
Theorem 3
and
Theorem 4
, we conclude that

\displaystyle\left(\beta_{0}-\widehat{\beta}\right)^{T}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{w}_{i}(x)^{2}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left(1-\widehat{\pi}_{i}\right)X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\left(\beta_{0}-\widehat{\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}+(1+r_{\ell})^{2}\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{4}}\right]\left[\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\phi_{s_{\beta}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left|\left|\mathbb{G}_{n}\right|\right|_{\mathcal{F}}\right]

\displaystyle=o_{P}(1)\left[o_{P}(1)+(1+r_{\ell})^{2}\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{4}}\right]\left[\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\phi_{s_{\beta}}+O_{P}(1)\right]

\displaystyle=o_{P}(1)

by our growth condition
\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\sqrt{\phi_{2s_{\ell}(x)}\phi_{2s_{\beta}}}=O(1)
again.
∎

E.6
Proof of
Theorem 7

Theorem 7

(Asymptotic normality of the debiased estimator)
.

Let
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
be a fixed query point and
s_{\max}=\max\left\{s_{\beta},s_{\ell}(x)\right\}
. Suppose that Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
,
4
,
5
, and
6
hold and
\lambda,\gamma,r_{\ell}>0
are specified as in
Theorem 3
(b) and
Theorem 4
, respectively. Furthermore, we assume that
\sigma_{\epsilon}\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\max}}^{3/2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}=O(1)
,

\displaystyle\frac{(1+\kappa_{R}^{2})s_{\max}\log(nd)}{\kappa_{R}^{4}}=o\left(\sqrt{n}\right),\quad\text{ and }\quad\frac{(1+\kappa_{R}^{4})\sqrt{\log(nd)}}{\kappa_{R}^{6}}\left(\sqrt{s_{\max}}\cdot r_{\ell}+s_{\max}r_{\pi}\right)=o(1).

Then, when
\sigma_{m}^{2}(x)=\lim_{n\to\infty}\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\cdot x^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x
exists, we have that

\sqrt{n}\left[\widehat{m}^{\mathrm{debias}}(x;\widehat{\bm{w}})-m_{0}(x)\right]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}\mathcal{N}\left(0,\,\sigma_{m}^{2}(x)\right).

Proof of
Theorem 7
.

We proceed our calculations in (
3.1
) and explicate the “Bias term” as:

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\left[\widehat{m}^{\text{debias}}(x;\widehat{\bm{w}})-m_{0}(x)\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\ell}(x)+\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\ell}(x)X_{i}+x\right]^{T}\sqrt{n}\left(\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)}_{\text{``Stochastic term''}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\ell_{0}(x)-\widehat{\ell}(x)\right]}_{\textbf{Bias I}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)X_{i}+x\right]^{T}\sqrt{n}\left(\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right)}_{\textbf{Bias II}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]X_{i}\right]^{T}\sqrt{n}\left(\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right)}_{\textbf{Bias III}}.

Notice that the “Stochastic term” can be written as:

-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x

according to
\ell_{0}(x)=-2\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x
in (
19
), where
R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x,i=1,...,n
are i.i.d. random variables with mean 0 and variance
\sigma_{m,d}^{2}(x)=\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\cdot x^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}(RXX^{T})\right]^{-1}x<\infty
. Moreover, the limit
\sigma_{m}^{2}(x)=\lim_{n\to\infty}\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\cdot x^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}(RXX^{T})\right]^{-1}x
exists and is finite by Assumption
4
. Now, we shall verify the condition of the Lyapunov central limit theorem. Specifically, each random variable
R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x
has a zero mean, is subexponential, and satisfies that

\displaystyle\left[\mathbb{E}\left|R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x\right|^{3}\right]^{\frac{1}{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\left|R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\left|\epsilon_{i}\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\left|\left|R_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{x^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}(RXX^{T})\right]^{-1}x}=\sigma_{m,d}(x)

by Proposition 2.7.1 and Lemma 2.7.7 in
Vershynin 2018
. Thus,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sigma_{m,d}^{3}(x)}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x\right|^{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}=o(1),

and the Lyapunov’s condition holds. As a result,

\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}\mathcal{N}\left(0,\,\sigma_{m}^{2}(x)\right).

It remains to show that “
\textbf{Bias I}+\textbf{Bias II}+\textbf{Bias III}
” is of order
o_{P}(1)
,
i.e.
, converging to 0 in probability.

Let
\delta\in(0,1)
. Notice that our assumptions guarantee that with probability at least
1-\delta
, the strong duality holds as well as

\displaystyle\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\beta},3),\quad\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)

\displaystyle\text{ and }\quad\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}\left(0,\,\frac{13r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{2\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{\nu}{\gamma}\right)\equiv\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),3\right)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1}),

recalling our arguments in
Theorem 3
,
Theorem 4
, Lemma
E.8
, and Lemma
E.9
. Moreover, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have that
r_{1}\equiv\frac{13r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{2\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{\nu}{\gamma}<1
under the condition in
Theorem 4
. In other words,

\left[\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),3\right)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\right]\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}=\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),3\right)\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}

with probability at least
1-\delta
.
We will use these results repeatedly in the following proof.

\bullet

Bias I:
We compute that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\textbf{Bias I}&=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\ell_{0}(x)-\widehat{\ell}(x)\right]\\
&=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\ell_{0}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\widehat{\ell}(x)\right]\\
&\leq\underbrace{\sqrt{n}\cdot\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\ell_{0}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]}_{\textbf{Term I}}\\
&\quad+\sqrt{n}\underbrace{\sup_{u\in\left[\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}\left(0,\,r_{1}\right)\right]\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}u\right|\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right|\right|_{2}}_{\textbf{Term II}},\end{split}

(45)

where
\widehat{\Delta}(x)=\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)
.

As for
Term I
, we note that under Assumptions
2
and
3
,
R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\ell_{0}(x)-\widehat{\ell}(x)\right],i=1,...,n
are i.i.d. centered sub-exponential random variables. In addition,

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\left|R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\ell_{0}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\left|R_{i}\epsilon_{i}\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\left|\left|X_{i}^{T}\left[\ell_{0}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\sigma_{\epsilon}\left\{\mathrm{E}\left[\left(\ell_{0}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right)^{T}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left(\ell_{0}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right)\right]\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\sigma_{\epsilon}\cdot\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap B_{d}\left(0,10r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\right)}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\sigma_{\epsilon}r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap B_{d}\left(0,1\right)}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}\cdot r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2},

where we use Lemma
E.13
in the first inequality, Lemma
E.8
in the third inequality, and Lemma
E.4
in the fifth inequality. By Bernstein’s inequality, we bound the probability

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\left(\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\ell_{0}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\left(-nA_{0}\min\left\{\frac{t^{2}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\cdot r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}},\frac{t}{\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}\cdot r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}}\right\}\right)

for some absolute constant
A_{0}>0
. It implies that

\displaystyle\textbf{Term I}\lesssim\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}\cdot r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left[\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}}\right].

As for
Term II
, by Assumptions
2
and
3
,
R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}v,i=1,...,n
are independent, centered, and sub-exponential random variables for all
v\in\left[\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}\left(0,\,r_{1}\right)\right]\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}
with

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\left|R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}v\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\left|R_{i}\epsilon_{i}\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\left|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}.

Thus, by Lemma
E.7
, union bound, and Bernstein’s inequality, we obtain that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in\left[\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}\left(0,\,r_{1}\right)\right]\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}\epsilon_{i}X_{i}^{T}v\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}\right].

Plugging the result back into (
45
), we conclude that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

Bias I

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}\cdot r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left[\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{n}\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}\left[\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}r_{\ell}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}+\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(d/s_{\ell}(x))}\right]

when
\log(1/\delta)=o(n)
and
s_{\max}\left(\log n+\log d\right)=o(\sqrt{n})
.

\bullet

Bias II:
Notice that

\textbf{Bias II}\leq\left|\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)X_{i}+x\right|\right|_{\infty}\sqrt{n}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right|\right|_{1}.

By
Theorem 3
, we know that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right|\right|_{1}\leq 4\sqrt{ns_{\beta}}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\phi_{1}}s_{\beta}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\sqrt{\log\left(d/\delta\right)}.

Additionally, by Lemma
E.12
, we know that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)X_{i}+x\right|\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}}}{\kappa_{R}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}}+\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}\right]

Thus, we obtain that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

Bias II

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\phi_{1}s_{\beta}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{3}}\cdot\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}},

provided that
s_{\max}\left(\log n+\log d\right)=o(\sqrt{n})
.

\bullet

Bias III:
Notice that

Bias III

\displaystyle=\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]X_{i}\right]^{T}\sqrt{n}\left(\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(R_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}-\mathrm{E}\left[R_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\right)\right]\sqrt{n}\left(\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(R_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}-\mathrm{E}\left[R_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right]\right)\right]\sqrt{n}\left(\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{n}}{2}\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{T}\mathrm{E}\left(R_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right)\left(\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right)+\frac{\sqrt{n}}{2}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{T}\mathrm{E}\left(R_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right)\left(\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\sqrt{n}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}\underbrace{\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\beta},3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\end{subarray}}\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{R_{i}(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)-\mathrm{E}\left[R_{i}(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)\right]\right\}\right|}_{\textbf{Term III}}

\displaystyle\quad+r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\sqrt{n}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}\underbrace{\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\beta},3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\end{subarray}}\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{R_{i}(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)-\mathrm{E}\left[R_{i}(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)\right]\right\}\right|}_{\textbf{Term IV}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{n}}{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}\underbrace{\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\beta},3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\end{subarray}}\left|\mathrm{E}\left[R_{i}(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)\right]\right|}_{\textbf{Term V}}

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\quad+\frac{\sqrt{n}}{2}r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}\underbrace{\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\beta},3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\end{subarray}}\left|\mathrm{E}\left[R_{i}(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)\right]\right|}_{\textbf{Term VI}}.\end{split}

(46)

By Lemmas
E.7
and
E.10
, we know that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\textbf{Term III},\,\textbf{Term IV}\lesssim\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\phi_{s_{\beta}}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\beta}\log(ed/s_{\beta})+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}

when
s_{\max}\left(\log n+\log d\right)=o(\sqrt{n})
and
\log(1/\delta)=o(n)
. By Lemma
E.4
, we can find discrete sets
M_{d,\ell},M_{d,\beta}\subset B_{d}(0,1)
with cardinalities
|M_{d,\ell}|\leq\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{5ed}{s_{\ell}(x)}\right)^{s_{\ell}(x)}
and
|M_{d,\beta}|\leq\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{5ed}{s_{\beta}}\right)^{s_{\beta}}
such that

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\subset 10\cdot\mathrm{conv}(M_{d,\ell}),\quad\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\subset 6\cdot\mathrm{conv}(M_{d,\ell}),

\displaystyle\text{ and }\quad\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\beta},3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\subset 10\cdot\mathrm{conv}(M_{d,\beta})

with
\left|\left|u\right|\right|_{0}\leq s_{\ell}(x),\left|\left|v\right|\right|_{0}\leq s_{\beta}
for all
u\in M_{d,\ell},v\in M_{d,\beta}
. Thus,

\displaystyle\textbf{Term V},\textbf{Term VI}\lesssim\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\phi_{s_{\beta}}}.

Again, we know from
Theorem 3
that with probability at least
1-\delta

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}-\beta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}\lesssim\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\sqrt{\phi_{1}s_{\beta}\log(d/\delta)}.

Thus, from (
E.6
), we conclude that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

Bias III

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\beta}}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}s_{\beta}\log(d/\delta)}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}+\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}r_{\ell}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[1+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\beta}\log(ed/s_{\beta})+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\beta}}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}s_{\beta}\log(d/\delta)}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}+\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}r_{\ell}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\right]

when
s_{\max}\left(\log n+\log d\right)=o(\sqrt{n})
and
\log(1/\delta)=o(n)
.

In summary, we can bound the bias terms with probability at least
1-\delta
as:

\displaystyle\textbf{Bias I}+\textbf{Bias II}+\textbf{Bias III}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}\left[\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}r_{\ell}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}+\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(d/s_{\ell}(x))}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\phi_{1}s_{\beta}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{3}}\cdot\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\beta}}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}s_{\beta}\log(d/\delta)}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}+\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}r_{\ell}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\right].

Recalling the result of
Theorem 4
that

\displaystyle\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\lesssim\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{3}}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}s_{\ell}(x)\log(d/\delta)}{n}}+\frac{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}{\kappa_{R}}\cdot r_{\ell}+\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}s_{\ell}(x)}}{\kappa_{R}}\cdot r_{\pi}\right]

and setting
\delta=\frac{1}{n}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\textbf{Bias I}+\textbf{Bias II}+\textbf{Bias III}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\phi_{1}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}s_{\beta}}{\kappa_{R}^{3}}\cdot\frac{\log(nd)}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O_{P}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\log n}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\cdot r_{\ell}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}\sqrt{\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\beta}}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}s_{\beta}\log(nd)}}{\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot r_{\ell}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O_{P}\left(\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left[\log n+s_{\ell}(x)\log(d/s_{\ell}(x))\right]}+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\beta}}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}s_{\beta}\log(nd)}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\right)\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\phi_{1}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}s_{\max}}{\kappa_{R}^{3}}\cdot\frac{\log(nd)}{\sqrt{n}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\max}}(1+\kappa_{R}^{2})\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{s_{\max}\log(nd)}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O_{P}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\max}}\log n}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\cdot r_{\ell}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}\left(1+\kappa_{R}^{2}\right)\sqrt{\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\max}}^{2}s_{\max}\log(nd)}}{\kappa_{R}^{6}}\cdot r_{\ell}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O_{P}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\max}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(1+\kappa_{R}^{2}\right)\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}s_{\max}\sqrt{\log(nd)}}{\kappa_{R}^{6}}\cdot r_{\pi}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\max}}(1+\kappa_{R}^{2})\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{s_{\max}\log(nd)}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O_{P}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\phi_{1}}\phi_{s_{\max}}\left(1+\kappa_{R}^{4}\right)\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}\sqrt{s_{\max}\log(nd)}}{\kappa_{R}^{6}}\cdot r_{\ell}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O_{P}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\max}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(1+\kappa_{R}^{2}\right)\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}s_{\max}\sqrt{\log(nd)}}{\kappa_{R}^{6}}\cdot r_{\pi}\right)

\displaystyle=o_{P}(1)

as long as
\sigma_{\epsilon}\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\max}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}=O(1)
,
\frac{(1+\kappa_{R}^{2})s_{\max}\log(nd)}{\kappa_{R}^{4}}=o\left(\sqrt{n}\right)
, and
\frac{(1+\kappa_{R}^{4})\sqrt{\log(nd)}}{\kappa_{R}^{6}}\left(\sqrt{s_{\max}}\cdot r_{\ell}+s_{\max}r_{\pi}\right)=o(1)
. The result follows.
∎

E.7
Proof of Proposition
8

Proposition 8

(Consistent estimate of the asymptotic variance)
.

Let
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
be a fixed query point. Suppose that Assumptions
1
,
2
,
4
,
5
, and
6
hold and
\gamma,r_{\ell}>0
are specified as in
Theorem 4
. Furthermore, we assume that
\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}^{2}=O(1)
,
\frac{(1+\kappa_{R}^{3})}{\kappa_{R}^{5}}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\ell}(x)\log(nd)}{n}}=o(1)
, and
\frac{(1+\kappa_{R}^{4})}{\kappa_{R}^{6}}\left[r_{\ell}+r_{\pi}\sqrt{s_{\ell}(x)}\right]=o(1)
. Then,

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\widehat{w}_{i}(x)^{2}-x^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x\right|=o_{P}(1).

Proof of Proposition
8
.

By Taylor’s expansion and the relations between the primal solution
\widehat{\bm{w}}(x)=\left(\widehat{w}_{1}(x),...,\widehat{w}_{n}(x)\right)^{T}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
and the dual solution
\widehat{\ell}(x)\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
in (
9
), we know that

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\widehat{w}_{i}(x)^{2}-x^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left[X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\ell}(x)\right]^{2}-x^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left[X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{2}-x^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x\right|+\left|\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widehat{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right)\right]^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})\left[X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{2}-x^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x\right|}_{\textbf{Term I}}+\underbrace{\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right)\left[X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{2}\right|}_{\textbf{Term II}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left|\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]\right|}_{\textbf{Term III}}+\underbrace{\left|\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right)X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]\right|}_{\textbf{Term IV}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right)\right]^{2}\right|}_{\textbf{Term V}}+\underbrace{\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right)\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right)\right]^{2}\right|}_{\textbf{Term VI}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right)\right]\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right)\right]\right|}_{\textbf{Term VII}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right)\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right)\right]\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right)\right]\right|}_{\textbf{Term VIII}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right)\right]^{2}\right|}_{\textbf{Term IX}}+\underbrace{\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right)\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right)\right]^{2}\right|}_{\textbf{Term X}}.

We know from Assumption
6
, Lemma
E.8
and Lemma
E.9
with
a_{0}=5
that

\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\leq r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2},\quad\ell_{0}(x)\in\mathcal{C}_{2}\left(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell}\right)

(47)

with
\vartheta_{\ell}=\frac{r_{\ell}}{\sqrt{1-r_{\ell}^{2}}}
, and

\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\in\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),3\right)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}\left(0,\,\frac{13r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{2\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{\nu}{\gamma}\right)\equiv\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),3\right)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1}),

(48)

where
B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r)=\left\{v\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:\left|\left|v\right|\right|_{1}\leq r\right\}
. Moreover, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have that
r_{1}\equiv\frac{13r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{2\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{\nu}{\gamma}<1
under the condition in
Theorem 4
. In other words,

\left[\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),3\right)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\right]\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}=\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),3\right)\bigcap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}

with probability at least
1-\delta
. Now, we bound these ten terms separately.

\bullet

Term I:
Note that
\ell_{0}(x)=-2\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x
and

\pi(X_{i})\left[X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{2}-x^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x,i=1,...,n

are independent, centered, and sub-exponential random variables with

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\left|\pi(X_{i})\left[X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{2}-x^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RXX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\left|\sqrt{\pi(X_{i})}\cdot X_{i}^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(\pi(X)XX^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}.

By Bernstein’s inequality (Corollary 2.8.3 in
Vershynin 2018
), we know that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

Term I

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})\left[X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{2}-x^{T}\left\{\mathrm{E}\left[\pi(X)XX^{T}\right]\right\}^{-1}x\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}}

when
\log(1/\delta)=o(n)
.

\bullet

Term II:
By (
47
) and Lemma
E.7
that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\textbf{Term II}=\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right)\left[X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell})\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{4}}\left\{\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell})\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|+\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell})\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{1}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{4}}\left[\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot r(n,\delta),

when
\log d=o(n)
and
\log(1/\delta)=o(n)
.

\bullet

Term III:
We know from the geometric constraints (
47
) and (
48
) as well as Lemmas
E.7
,
E.8
, and
E.10
that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

Term III

\displaystyle=\left|\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]\right|+\left|\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\pi(X_{i})X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)-\mathrm{E}\left[\pi(X_{i})X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]\right\}\right]^{T}\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\pi(X_{i})X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\pi(X_{i})X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)-\mathrm{E}\left[\pi(X_{i})X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]\right\}\right]^{T}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{T}\mathrm{E}\left[\pi(X_{i})X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell})\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\pi(X_{i})(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)-\mathrm{E}\left[\pi(X_{i})(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 128.0374pt+\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\pi(X_{i})(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)\right]\right|\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\end{subarray}}\Bigg\{\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\pi(X_{i})(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)-\mathrm{E}\left[\pi(X_{i})(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 128.0374pt+\left|\mathrm{E}\left[\pi(X_{i})(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)\right]\right|\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left[\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}+r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\right]\left[\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}+\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}r_{\ell}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\right]

when
\log d=o(n)
and
\log(1/\delta)=o(n)
.

\bullet

Term IV:
By Assumption
5
, Lemmas
E.4
,
E.7
, and
E.10
, we obtain that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\textbf{Term IV}=\left|\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right)X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{T}\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\Biggl[\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\cdot X_{i}^{T}v\right|+\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\cdot X_{i}^{T}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]\right|\Biggr]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\cdot\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\Biggl[\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\\
u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell})\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}u\cdot X_{i}^{T}v\right|

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sup_{u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell})\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}u\cdot X_{i}^{T}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]\right|\Biggr]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\lesssim}}\frac{r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\cdot\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}+\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}+\sup_{u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell})\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{T}u\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\cdot\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}+\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\times\Biggl\{\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\right]\right\}+\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\mathrm{E}\left[\left(X_{1}^{T}v\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sup_{u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell})\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\left(X_{i}^{T}u\right)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[\left(X_{i}^{T}u\right)^{2}\right]\right\}+\sup_{u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell})\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\mathrm{E}\left[\left(X_{1}^{T}u\right)^{2}\right]\Biggr\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\cdot\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\left(\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}+\frac{r_{\ell}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\right)\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left[\sqrt{\frac{s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+1\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\cdot\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\left(\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}+\frac{r_{\ell}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\right)\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}

when
\log d=o(n)
and
\log(1/\delta)=o(n)
, where we use the fact that
|a|\cdot|b|\leq\frac{a^{2}+b^{2}}{2}
in the (asymptotic) inequality (i).

\bullet

Term V and VI:
Under (
48
), Assumption
5
, Lemmas
E.4
,
E.7
, and
E.10
, we have that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\textbf{Term V}+\textbf{Term VI}=\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right)\right]^{2}\right|+\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right)\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right)\right]^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\right)\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right)\right]^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\right)\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\sup_{v\in\left[\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\cup\{e_{1},...,e_{d}\}\right]\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\left[\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|+\left|\frac{1}{4n}\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{1}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(1+r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\right)\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+1\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(1+r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\right)\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}

when
\log d=o(n)
and
\log(1/\delta)=o(n)
.

\bullet

Term VII:
Under the geometric constraints (
47
), (
48
), Lemmas
E.7
,
E.8
, and
E.10
, we know that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\textbf{Term VII}=\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right)\right]\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\end{subarray}}\left[\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\pi(X_{i})(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[\pi(X_{i})(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|+\left|\frac{1}{2n}\mathrm{E}\left[\pi(X_{1})(X_{1}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}r_{\ell}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\cdot\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+1\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}r_{\ell}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\cdot\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}

when
\log d=o(n)
and
\log(1/\delta)=o(n)
.

\bullet

Term VIII:
We bound
Term VIII
using the similar arguments as
Term IV
as:

\displaystyle\textbf{Term VIII}=\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right)\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right)\right]\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Biggl\{\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[\left|X_{i}^{T}v\cdot X_{i}^{T}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]\right|-\mathrm{E}\left|X_{i}^{T}v\cdot X_{i}^{T}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\ell}(x),3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\mathrm{E}\left|X_{i}^{T}v\cdot X_{i}^{T}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]\right|\Biggr\}

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\cdot\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\frac{r_{\ell}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\cdot\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}

with probability at least
1-\delta
when
\log d=o(n)
and
\log(1/\delta)=o(n)
.

\bullet

Term IX and X:
Under (
47
), Assumption
5
, Lemma
E.8
, and Lemma
E.7
, we have that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\textbf{Term IX}+\textbf{Term X}=\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi(X_{i})\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right)\right]^{2}\right|+\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right)\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right)\right]^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\right)\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[X_{i}^{T}\left(\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right)\right]^{2}\right|
