\displaystyle\leq\left(1+r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\right)r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\left[\left|\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|+\left|\frac{1}{4n}\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{1}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(1+r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\right)\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}r_{\ell}^{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{\ell}(x)\log(ed/s_{\ell}(x))}{n}}+1\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(1+r_{\pi}(n,\delta)\right)\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}r_{\ell}^{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}

when
\log d=o(n)
and
\log(1/\delta)=o(n)
.

\bullet

Conclusion:
Combining our above calculations yields that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\widehat{w}_{i}(x)^{2}-x^{T}\left\{\mathrm{E}\left[\pi(X_{1})X_{1}X_{1}^{T}\right]\right\}^{-1}x\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{n}}+\frac{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{4}}\left(r_{\pi}+r_{\ell}+r_{\pi}r_{\ell}+r_{\ell}^{2}+r_{\pi}r_{\ell}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\left(1+r_{\ell}+r_{\pi}r_{\ell}\right)+\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left(1+r_{\pi}\right)\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}.

By setting
\delta=\frac{1}{nd}
with our bound for
\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}
in
Theorem 4
, we conclude that

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\widehat{w}_{i}(x)^{2}-x^{T}\left\{\mathrm{E}\left[\pi(X_{1})X_{1}X_{1}^{T}\right]\right\}^{-1}x\right|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(nd)}{n}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{4}}\left(r_{\pi}+r_{\ell}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O_{P}\left(\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left[1+r_{\pi}+\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}(1+r_{\ell})\right]\right)\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(nd)}{n}}\right)+O_{P}\left(\frac{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{4}}\left(r_{\pi}+r_{\ell}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O_{P}\left(\frac{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{3}}\left[1+r_{\pi}+\frac{\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}(1+r_{\ell})\right]\left[\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}s_{\ell}(x)\log(nd)}{n}}+\frac{\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}r_{\ell}}{\kappa_{R}}+\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}s_{\ell}(x)}}{\kappa_{R}}\cdot r_{\pi}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=o_{P}(1)

when
\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}^{2}=O(1)
,
\frac{(1+\kappa_{R}^{3})}{\kappa_{R}^{5}}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\ell}(x)\log(nd)}{n}}=o(1)
, and
\frac{(1+\kappa_{R}^{4})}{\kappa_{R}^{6}}\left[r_{\ell}+r_{\pi}\sqrt{s_{\ell}(x)}\right]=o(1)
.
Therefore,
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\widehat{w}_{i}(x)^{2}
is a consistent estimator of the asymptotic variance
x^{T}\left[\mathrm{E}\left(RX_{1}X_{1}^{T}\right)\right]^{-1}x
.
∎

E.8
Proof of Proposition
C.1

Before presenting the proof of Proposition
C.1
, we first establish the consistency of
\widehat{\theta}
from the Lasso-type generalized linear regression in the following lemma, which has its own interest.

Lemma E.2

(Consistency of propensity score estimation with Lasso-type generalized linear regression)
.

Let
\delta\in(0,1)
and
A_{1},A_{2},A_{3}>0
be some absolute constants. Suppose that Assumptions
2
,
7
,
8
, and
9
hold as well as

d\geq s_{\theta}+2,\quad A_{1}\phi_{s_{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{\phi_{s_{\theta}}}{\rho}\right)\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}s_{\theta}\log d}{n}}\leq\frac{\rho}{8},\;\text{ and }\;A_{2}\exp\left[-\frac{n\rho^{2}}{\phi_{s_{\theta}}^{2}\log^{2}\left(\frac{\phi_{s_{\theta}}}{\rho}\right)}-\phi_{1}s_{\theta}\log d\right]\leq\delta.

(a)

If
\zeta\geq\left|\left|\frac{2}{n}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})-R_{i}\right]X_{i}}{\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\right]}\right|\right|_{\infty}
, then with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\left|\left|\widehat{\theta}-\theta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{s_{\theta}}\zeta}{\rho}.

(b)

If, in addition,
\zeta=A_{3}A_{\varphi}\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}\log(d/\delta)}{n}}
, then with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\left|\left|\widehat{\theta}-\theta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}\lesssim\frac{A_{\varphi}}{\rho}\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}s_{\theta}\log(d/\delta)}{n}}.

Proof of Lemma
E.2
.

The proof is partially adopted from Theorem 2 in
Cai et al. 2021
.

(a) Since
\widehat{\theta}
minimizes the objective function in (
27
), we know that

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}\log\left[\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\theta})\right]-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-R_{i})\log\left[1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\theta})\right]+\zeta\left|\left|\widehat{\theta}\right|\right|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}R_{i}\log\left[\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\right]-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-R_{i})\log\left[1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\right]+\zeta\left|\left|\theta_{0}\right|\right|_{1}.

By Taylor’s expansion on
\mathcal{L}_{\varphi}(\theta)
at
\theta_{0}
in the direction of
\widehat{\Delta}=\widehat{\theta}-\theta_{0}
, we have that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\cdot\widehat{\Delta}^{T}\left[\nabla\nabla\mathcal{L}_{\varphi}(\theta_{0}+t\widehat{\Delta})\right]\widehat{\Delta}\,dt&\leq-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{\varphi}(\theta_{0})^{T}\widehat{\Delta}+\zeta\left[\left|\left|\theta_{0}\right|\right|_{1}-\left|\left|\theta_{0}+\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{1}\right].\end{split}

(49)

By Assumption
7
(c), we know that the left-hand side of (
49
) can be lower bounded as:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\cdot\widehat{\Delta}^{T}\left[\nabla\nabla\mathcal{L}_{\varphi}(\theta_{0}+t\widehat{\Delta})\right]\widehat{\Delta}\,dt

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\cdot H(\theta_{0}+t\widehat{\Delta};R_{i},X_{i})\left(X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\Delta}\right)^{2}dt>0.

It thus implies that

-\nabla\mathcal{L}_{\varphi}(\theta_{0})^{T}\widehat{\Delta}+\zeta\left[\left|\left|\theta_{0}\right|\right|_{1}-\left|\left|\theta_{0}+\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{1}\right]>0.

(50)

In addition, we know that

\displaystyle\nabla\mathcal{L}_{\varphi}(\theta_{0})

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{R_{i}X_{i}\cdot\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})}{\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{(1-R_{i})X_{i}\cdot\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})}{1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})-R_{i}\right]X_{i}}{\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\right]}.

By Hölder’s inequality and the (reverse) triangle’s inequality together with the sparsity of
\theta_{0}
under Assumption
9
, we know from (
50
) that

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left|\nabla\mathcal{L}_{\varphi}(\theta_{0})\right|\right|_{\infty}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{1}+\zeta\left[\left|\left|\theta_{0,S_{\theta}}\right|\right|_{1}-\left|\left|\theta_{0,S_{\theta}}+\widehat{\Delta}_{S_{\theta}}\right|\right|_{1}-\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}_{S_{\theta}^{c}}\right|\right|_{1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\zeta}{2}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}_{S_{\theta}}\right|\right|_{1}+\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}_{S_{\theta}^{c}}\right|\right|_{1}\right]+\zeta\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}_{S_{\theta}}\right|\right|_{1}-\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}_{S_{\theta}^{c}}\right|\right|_{1}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{3\zeta}{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}_{S_{\theta}}\right|\right|_{1}-\frac{\zeta}{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}_{S_{\theta}^{c}}\right|\right|_{1},

where
\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}_{S_{\theta}}\right]\right|\right|_{1}=\sum_{k\in S_{\theta}}\left|\widehat{\Delta}_{k}\right|
. It implies that
\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}_{S_{\theta}^{c}}\right|\right|_{1}\leq 3\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}_{S_{\theta}}\right|\right|_{1}
and thus, the estimation error
\widehat{\Delta}=\widehat{\theta}-\theta_{0}
lies in the cone
\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\theta},3)
.

Now, by Assumption
9
(c), we know that for all
1\leq i\leq n
,

\left|\log H(\theta_{0}+t\widehat{\Delta};R_{i},X_{i})-\log H(\theta_{0};R_{i},X_{i})\right|\leq A_{H}\left(|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|^{2}+t^{2}|X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\Delta}|^{2}+t|X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\Delta}|\right),

which implies that for all
1\leq i\leq n
,

\displaystyle\exp\left[-A_{H}\left(|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|^{2}+t^{2}|X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\Delta}|^{2}+t|X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\Delta}|\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{H(\theta_{0}+t\widehat{\Delta};R_{i},X_{i})}{H(\theta_{0};R_{i},X_{i})}

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left[A_{H}\left(|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|^{2}+t^{2}|X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\Delta}|^{2}+t|X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\Delta}|\right)\right].

Let
K_{d}(S_{\theta})=\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\theta},3)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}
. Thus, for two constants
T_{1},T_{2}>0
that will be determined in Lemma
E.3
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\cdot H(\theta_{0}+t\widehat{\Delta};R_{i},X_{i})\left(X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\Delta}\right)^{2}dt

\displaystyle\geq\inf_{v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\cdot H(\theta_{0}+tv;R_{i},X_{i})\cdot\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}dt\right]\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\inf_{v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\cdot H(\theta_{0};R_{i},X_{i})\exp\left[-A_{H}\left(|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|^{2}+t^{2}|X_{i}^{T}v|^{2}+t|X_{i}^{T}v|\right)\right]\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}dt\right\}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\geq}}\inf_{v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})}\Big\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\cdot H(\theta_{0};R_{i},X_{i})\exp\left[-A_{H}\left(|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|^{2}+t^{2}|X_{i}^{T}v|^{2}+t|X_{i}^{T}v|\right)\right]\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\times\mathbbm{1}_{\{\sqrt{A_{H}}\cdot t|X_{i}^{T}v|\leq T_{1}\}}dt\Big\}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{\geq}}\inf_{v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\cdot H(\theta_{0};R_{i},X_{i})\exp\left[-A_{H}|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|^{2}-T_{1}^{2}-\sqrt{A_{H}}T_{1}\right]\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{\sqrt{A_{H}}\cdot t|X_{i}^{T}v|\leq T_{1}\right\}}dt\right\}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{\geq}}\inf_{v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\cdot H(\theta_{0};R_{i},X_{i})\exp\left[-A_{H}|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|^{2}-T_{1}^{2}-\sqrt{A_{H}}T_{1}\right]\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{\sqrt{A_{H}}|X_{i}^{T}v|\leq T_{1}\right\}}dt\right\}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\inf_{v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})}\left\{\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}H(\theta_{0};R_{i},X_{i})\exp\left[-A_{H}|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|^{2}-T_{1}^{2}-\sqrt{A_{H}}T_{1}\right]\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{\sqrt{A_{H}}|X_{i}^{T}v|\leq T_{1}\right\}}\right\}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\inf_{v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})}\left\{\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}H(\theta_{0};R_{i},X_{i})\exp\left[-A_{H}T_{2}^{2}-T_{1}^{2}-\sqrt{A_{H}}T_{1}\right]\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{\sqrt{A_{H}}|X_{i}^{T}v|\leq T_{1}\right\}}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|\leq T_{2}\}}\right\}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{2}^{2},

where we multiple the indicator
\mathbbm{1}_{\{\sqrt{A_{H}}\cdot t|X_{i}^{T}v|\leq T_{1}\}}\in\{0,1\}\leq 1
to derive (i), plugging in the upper bound
T_{1}
in the indicator
\mathbbm{1}_{\{\sqrt{A_{H}}\cdot t|X_{i}^{T}v|\leq T_{1}\}}
in the inequality (ii), and take the largest value
t=1
in
\mathbbm{1}_{\{\sqrt{A_{H}}\cdot t|X_{i}^{T}v|\leq T_{1}\}}
to obtain (iii). Notice that by Assumption
9
(a,c), there exists some constant
c_{\bm{T}}^{\prime}>0
that only depends on
\bm{T}=(T_{1},T_{2})
such that in the region
\left\{X_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|\leq T_{2}\right\}
,

H(\theta_{0};R_{i},X_{i})\geq c_{\bm{T}}^{\prime}

for all
i=1,...,n
. In detail, this is because

\displaystyle H(\theta_{0};R_{i},X_{i})\geq H(0;R_{i},X_{i})\exp\left(-A_{H}|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|^{2}\right),

while
H(0;R_{i},X_{i})>0
and
X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}
is bounded within the region
\left\{X_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|\leq T_{2}\right\}
. Therefore, we can proceed the above display as:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\cdot H(\theta_{0}+t\widehat{\Delta};R_{i},X_{i})\left(X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\Delta}\right)^{2}dt

\displaystyle\geq\inf_{v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})}\left\{\frac{c_{\bm{T}}^{\prime}}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\exp\left(-A_{H}T_{2}^{2}-T_{1}^{2}-\sqrt{A_{H}}T_{1}\right)\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{\sqrt{A_{H}}|X_{i}^{T}v|\leq T_{1}\right\}}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|\leq T_{2}\right\}}\right\}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\equiv\inf_{v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})}\left[\frac{C_{\bm{T}}}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{\sqrt{A_{H}}|X_{i}^{T}v|\leq T_{1}\right\}}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|\leq T_{2}\right\}}\right]\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{2}^{2},

where
C_{\bm{T}}=\frac{c_{\bm{T}}^{\prime}}{2}\exp\left(-A_{H}T_{2}^{2}-T_{1}^{2}-\sqrt{A_{H}}T_{1}\right)>0
is a constant that depends on
A_{H}>0
(thus, the link function
\varphi
) and the values of
\bm{T}=(T_{1},T_{2})
. In addition, we consider the truncation function with
T_{1}>0
such that

h_{T_{1}}(u)=\begin{cases}u^{2}&\text{ if }0\leq|u|\leq\frac{T_{1}}{2\sqrt{A_{H}}},\\
\left(\frac{T_{1}}{\sqrt{A_{H}}}-u\right)^{2}&\text{ if }\frac{T_{1}}{2\sqrt{A_{H}}}\leq|u|\leq\frac{T_{1}}{\sqrt{A_{H}}},\\
0&\text{ otherwise},\end{cases}

(51)

and denote that
f_{v}(x)=x^{T}v\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|\leq T_{2}\right\}}
and
g_{v}(x)=h_{T_{1}}\left(f_{v}(x)\right)
. Then, we know that

\displaystyle\inf_{v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})}\left[\frac{C_{\bm{T}}}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{\sqrt{A_{H}}|X_{i}^{T}v|\leq T_{1}\right\}}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|\leq T_{2}\right\}}\right]\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\inf_{v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})}\left[\frac{C_{\bm{T}}}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{v}(X_{i})\right]\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}.

By Lemma
E.3
below, we can take

T_{1}=\bar{A}_{3}\sqrt{A_{H}\phi_{s_{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{\phi_{s_{\theta}}}{\rho}\right)}\quad\text{ and }\quad T_{2}=\bar{A}_{4}\left|\left|\theta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{\phi_{s_{\theta}}}{\rho}\right)}

so that

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\left(\inf_{v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta},\vartheta),\left|\left|v\right|\right|_{1}=t}\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[g_{v}(X)\right]\geq\frac{\rho}{4}-\bar{A}_{1}K_{1}t\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}\log d}{n}}\right)\leq\bar{A}_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{n\rho^{2}}{K_{1}^{2}}-\phi_{1}t^{2}\log d\right)

with
K_{1}=\bar{A}_{3}\phi_{s_{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{\phi_{s_{\theta}}}{\rho}\right)
, where
\bar{A}_{1},\bar{A}_{2},\bar{A}_{3},\bar{A}_{4}>0
are some absolute constants. Notice that for any
v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})
, we know that

1\leq\left|\left|v\right|\right|_{1}\leq 4\left|\left|v_{S_{\theta}}\right|\right|_{1}\leq 4\sqrt{s_{\theta}}\left|\left|v\right|\right|_{2}=4\sqrt{s_{\theta}}.

Therefore, under the condition that

A_{1}\phi_{s_{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{\phi_{s_{\theta}}}{\rho}\right)\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}s_{\theta}\log d}{n}}\leq\frac{\rho}{8}\quad\text{ and }\quad A_{2}\exp\left[-\frac{n\rho^{2}}{\phi_{s_{\theta}}^{2}\log^{2}\left(\frac{\phi_{s_{\theta}}}{\rho}\right)}-\phi_{1}s_{\theta}\log d\right]\leq\delta

for some absolute constants
A_{1},A_{2}>0
, we have that with probability at least
1-\delta

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\cdot H(\theta_{0}+t\widehat{\Delta})\left(X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\Delta}\right)^{2}dt

\displaystyle\geq\inf_{v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})}\left[\frac{C_{\bm{T}}}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{\sqrt{A_{H}}|X_{i}^{T}v|\leq T_{1}\right\}}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|\leq T_{2}\right\}}\right]\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\rho}{8}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}.

Substituting the above inequality to (
49
) yields that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\frac{\rho}{8}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\zeta}{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{1}+\zeta\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}_{S_{\theta}}\right|\right|_{1}-\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}_{S_{\theta}^{c}}\right|\right|_{1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3\zeta}{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}_{S_{\theta}}\right|\right|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3\zeta\sqrt{s_{\theta}}}{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{2}.

The result follows by dividing
\left|\left|\widehat{\Delta}\right|\right|_{2}
on both side of the above inequality and some rearrangements.

(b) Notice that by law of total expectation,

\mathrm{E}\left\{\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})-R_{i}\right]X_{i}}{\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\right]}\right\}=\mathrm{E}\left\{\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{X_{i}\cdot\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\cdot\mathrm{E}\left[\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})-R_{i}|X_{i}\right]}{\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\right]}\right\}=0.

In addition,

\left|\left|\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})-R_{i}\right]X_{i}}{\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\right]}\right|\right|_{\infty}=\max_{1\leq k\leq d}\left|\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})-R_{i}\right]}{\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\right]}\cdot X_{ik}\right|

and by (a) and (b) in Assumption
9
together with Remark
C.1
, we know that

\displaystyle\frac{\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})-R_{i}\right]}{\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\right]}\cdot X_{ik}

\displaystyle=\left[\frac{R_{i}\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})}{\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})}-\frac{(1-R_{i})\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})}{1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})}\right]X_{ik}

\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{\frac{\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})}{\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})},\,\frac{\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})}{1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})}\right\}|X_{ik}|

\displaystyle\leq A_{\varphi}|X_{ik}|,

showing that for any fixed
k
,
\frac{\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})-R_{i}\right]}{\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\right]}\cdot X_{ik},i=1,...,n
are independent and sub-Gaussian random variables with zero means. By Assumptions
2
, we also have that

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq k\leq d}\left|\left|\frac{\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})-R_{i}\right]}{\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\right]}X_{ik}\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{1\leq k\leq d}A_{\varphi}\left|\left|X_{ik}\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim A_{\varphi}\sqrt{\phi_{1}}

for all
1\leq i\leq n
. Therefore, by the general Hoeffding’s inequality (Theorem 2.6.2 in
Vershynin 2018
), we know that there exists an absolute constant
A_{0}>0
such that for all
t>0
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\left(\left|\left|\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{X_{i}\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})-R_{i}\right]}{\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\right]}\right|\right|_{\infty}>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq d\cdot\max_{1\leq k\leq d}\mathrm{P}\left(\left|\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})-R_{i}\right]}{\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\right]}\cdot X_{ik}\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2d\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{nA_{0}t^{2}}{A_{\varphi}\phi_{1}}\right).

Hence, we conclude that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{X_{i}\varphi^{\prime}(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})-R_{i}\right]}{\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\left[1-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\right]}\right|\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim A_{\varphi}\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}\log(d/\delta)}{n}}.

Finally, the bound on
\left|\left|\widehat{\theta}-\theta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}
follows from (a), where we substitute
\zeta=A_{3}A_{\varphi}\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}\log(d/\delta)}{n}}
for some absolute constant
A_{3}>0
into the upper bound of
\left|\left|\widehat{\theta}-\theta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}
.
∎

Lemma E.3
.

Let
\bar{A}_{1},\bar{A}_{2},\bar{A}_{3}>0
be two absolute constants. Under Assumptions
2
and
8
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\left(\inf_{v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta},\vartheta),\left|\left|v\right|\right|_{1}=t}\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[g_{v}(X)\right]\leq\frac{\rho}{4}-\bar{A}_{1}K_{1}t\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}\log d}{n}}\right)\leq\bar{A}_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{n\rho^{2}}{K_{1}^{2}}-\phi_{1}t^{2}\log d\right),

where
\mathbb{P}_{n}
is the empirical expectation over
n
covariate vectors
\{X_{1},...,X_{n}\}
,
K_{d}(S_{\theta})=\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\theta},\vartheta)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}
,
K_{1}=\bar{A}_{3}\phi_{s_{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{\phi_{s_{\theta}}}{\rho}\right)
, and the function
g_{v}
is defined in (
51
).

Proof of Lemma
E.3
.

The proof is partially adopted from Theorem 2 and Lemma 4 in
Cai et al. 2021
as well as Proposition 3 in
Negahban et al. 2010
. We prove this concentration bound with the following two steps.

1.

For any
v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})=\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\theta},\vartheta)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}
, we show that

\mathrm{E}\left[g_{v}(X)\right]\geq\frac{\rho}{2}.

(52)

2.

Define the random variable

Z(t)=\sup_{v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta},\vartheta),\left|\left|v\right|\right|_{1}=t}\big|\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[g_{v}(X)\right]-\mathrm{E}\left[g_{v}(X)\right]\big|.

Then, it holds that

\mathrm{P}\left(Z(t)\geq\frac{\rho}{4}+\bar{A}_{1}K_{1}t\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}\log d}{n}}\right)\leq\bar{A}_{2}\exp\left(-\frac{n\rho^{2}}{K_{1}^{2}}-\phi_{1}t^{2}\log d\right),

(53)

where
K_{1}=\bar{A}_{3}^{2}\phi_{s_{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{4\phi_{s_{\theta}}}{\rho}\right)
and
\bar{A}_{1},\bar{A}_{2},\bar{A}_{3}>0
are two absolute constants.

Combining (
52
) and (
53
) yields the final result, so it remains to prove these two steps.

Proof of (
52
).
By Assumption
4
(more precisely, Eq.(
17
)) on
\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\theta},3)
, we know that
\mathrm{E}\left[(X^{T}v)^{2}\right]\geq\rho\left|\left|v\right|\right|_{2}^{2}=\rho
for any
v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})=\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\theta},\vartheta)\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}
. Then, it suffices to show that

\mathrm{E}\left[(X^{T}v)^{2}-g_{v}(X)\right]\leq\frac{\rho}{2}.

Note that
g_{v}(X)=(X^{T}v)^{2}
when
|X^{T}\theta_{0}|\leq T_{2}
and
|X^{T}v|\leq\frac{T_{1}}{2\sqrt{A_{H}}}
. Thus, by the union bound, we have that

\mathrm{E}\left[(X^{T}v)^{2}-g_{v}(X)\right]\leq\mathrm{E}\left[\left(X^{T}v\right)^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{|X^{T}\theta_{0}|\geq T_{2}\right\}}\right]+\mathrm{E}\left[\left(X^{T}v\right)^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{|X^{T}v|\geq\frac{T_{1}}{2\sqrt{A_{H}}}\right\}}\right].

Under Assumption
2
, both
X^{T}v
and
X^{T}\theta_{0}
are sub-Gaussian random variables. By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and the sub-Gaussian tail bound, we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left(X^{T}v\right)^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{|X^{T}\theta_{0}|\geq T_{2}\right\}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\mathrm{E}\left[\left(X^{T}v\right)^{4}\right]}\cdot\sqrt{\mathrm{P}\left(|X^{T}\theta_{0}|\geq T_{2}\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\left(2\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\theta}}}\right)^{4}}\cdot\sqrt{2\exp\left(-\frac{T_{2}^{2}}{\left|\left|\theta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\phi_{s_{\theta}}}\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\phi_{s_{\theta}}\exp\left(-\frac{T_{2}^{2}}{\left|\left|\theta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}^{2}\phi_{s_{\theta}}}\right)

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\left(X^{T}v\right)^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{|X^{T}v|\geq\frac{T_{1}}{2\sqrt{A_{H}}}\right\}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\mathrm{E}\left[\left(X^{T}v\right)^{4}\right]}\cdot\sqrt{\mathrm{P}\left(\left|X^{T}v\right|\geq\frac{T_{1}}{2\sqrt{A_{H}}}\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\phi_{s_{\theta}}\exp\left(-\frac{T_{1}^{2}}{A_{H}\phi_{s_{\theta}}}\right)

for
v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})
, where
\phi_{s_{\theta}}=\sup_{\left|\left|v\right|\right|_{0}\leq s,v\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1}}\mathrm{E}\left[\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\right]=\sup_{v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})}\mathrm{E}\left[\left(X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{2}\right]
by Lemma
E.4
.
Therefore, we can take
T_{1}=\bar{A}_{3}\sqrt{A_{H}\phi_{s_{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{\phi_{s_{\theta}}}{\rho}\right)}
and
T_{2}=\bar{A}_{4}\left|\left|\theta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{\phi_{s_{\theta}}}{\rho}\right)}
for some absolute constants
\bar{A}_{3},\bar{A}_{4}>0
in order for both of the above two expectations to be less than
\frac{\rho}{4}
. It thus proves (
52
).

Proof of (
53
).
For any
v\in K_{d}(S_{\theta})
, we know that
\left|\left|g_{v}(X)\right|\right|_{\infty}\leq\frac{T_{1}^{2}}{A_{H}}=\bar{A}_{3}^{2}\phi_{s_{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{\phi_{s_{\theta}}}{\rho}\right):=K_{1}
for some absolute constant
\bar{A}_{3}>0
. By the bounded differences inequality (Theorem 2.9.1 in
Vershynin 2018
), we know that

\mathrm{P}\left(Z(t)\geq\mathrm{E}\left[Z(t)\right]+z\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{nz^{2}}{2K_{1}^{2}}\right)

for any
z>0
. Taking
z^{*}=\frac{\rho}{4}+K_{1}t\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}\log d}{n}}
yields that

\mathrm{P}\left(Z(t)\geq\mathrm{E}\left[Z(t)\right]+z^{*}\right)\leq\exp\left[-\frac{n}{2K_{1}^{2}}\left(\frac{\rho}{4}+K_{1}t\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}\log d}{n}}\right)^{2}\right].

Hence, in order to prove (
53
), it suffices to show that

\mathrm{E}\left[Z(t)\right]\lesssim K_{1}t\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}\log d}{n}}.

(54)

Given a sequence
\{\omega_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
of i.i.d. Rademacher random variables, the standard symmetrization argument (Lemma 6.4.2 in
Vershynin 2018
) gives that

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[Z(t)\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathrm{E}\left[\sup_{v\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1},\left|\left|v\right|\right|_{1}=t}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\omega_{i}\,g_{v}(X_{i})\right|\right]

\displaystyle=2\mathrm{E}\left[\sup_{v\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1},\left|\left|v\right|\right|_{1}=t}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\omega_{i}\,h_{T_{1}}\left(f_{v}(X_{i})\right)\right|\right].

By the definition (
51
), the function
h_{T_{1}}
is Lipschitz with parameter at most
\frac{T_{1}}{\sqrt{A_{H}}}=\sqrt{K_{1}}
, which can be chosen to be smaller than
K_{1}
when we potentially enlarge
K_{1}
to
\max\left\{1,\bar{A}_{3}^{2}\phi_{s_{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{4\phi_{s_{\theta}}}{\rho}\right)\right\}
up to an absolute constant. In addition,
h_{T_{1}}(0)=0
. Therefore, by the contraction principle (Theorem 6.7.1 in
Vershynin 2018
), we have that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathrm{E}\left[Z(t)\right]&\leq 4K_{1}\mathrm{E}\left[\sup_{v\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1},\left|\left|v\right|\right|_{1}=t}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\omega_{i}\,f_{v}(X_{i})\right|\right]\\
&=4K_{1}\mathrm{E}\left[\sup_{v\in\mathbb{S}^{d-1},\left|\left|v\right|\right|_{1}=t}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\omega_{i}\cdot X_{i}^{T}v\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|\leq T_{2}\right\}}\right|\right]\\
&\leq 4K_{1}t\cdot\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\omega_{i}\cdot X_{i}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|\leq T_{2}\right\}}\right|\right|_{\infty}\right],\end{split}

(55)

where the last inequality is due to Hölder’s inequality. Finally, since
\omega_{i}\cdot X_{i}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|\leq T_{2}\right\}},i=1,...,n
are centered independent sub-Gaussian random vectors, we apply the standard bounds for sub-Gaussian maxima to obtain that

\mathrm{E}\left[\left|\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\omega_{i}\cdot X_{i}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{|X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0}|\leq T_{2}\right\}}\right|\right|_{\infty}\right]\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}\log d}{n}}.

Combining with (
55
) leads to (
54
), and (
53
) is thus proved.
∎

Proposition C.1
.

Let
\delta\in(0,1)
and
A_{1},A_{2},A_{3},A_{4}>0
be some absolute constants. Suppose that Assumptions
2
,
7
,
8
, and
9
hold as well as

d\geq s_{\theta}+2,\quad A_{1}\phi_{s_{\theta}}\log\left(\frac{\phi_{s_{\theta}}}{\rho}\right)\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}s_{\theta}\log d}{n}}\leq\frac{\rho}{8},\;\text{ and }\;A_{2}\exp\left[-\frac{n\rho^{2}}{\phi_{s_{\theta}}^{2}\log^{2}\left(\frac{\phi_{s_{\theta}}}{\rho}\right)}-\phi_{1}s_{\theta}\log d\right]\leq\delta.

If
\zeta=A_{3}A_{\varphi}\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}\log(d/\delta)}{n}}
, then the rate of consistency
r_{\pi}=r_{\pi}(n,\delta)
in Assumption
5
can be taken as:

r_{\pi}(n,\delta)=\frac{A_{4}L_{\varphi}A_{\varphi}}{\rho}\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\theta}}s_{\theta}\log(d/\delta)}{n}\left[\log\left(\frac{n}{\delta}\right)+s_{\theta}\log\left(\frac{ed}{s_{\theta}}\right)\right]}.

Proof of Proposition
C.1
.

Under the differentiability and Lipschitz conditions on the link function
\varphi
guaranteed by Assumption
9
, we can utilize the Taylor’s theorem to obtain that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left|\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right|&=\left|\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\theta})-\varphi(X_{i}^{T}\theta_{0})\right|\\
&\leq L_{\varphi}\left(\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\theta},3)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|\right)\left|\left|\widehat{\theta}-\theta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}\end{split}

(56)

for all
i=1,...,n
with probability at least
1-\delta
, where the last inequality follows from the proof of Lemma
E.2
that
\widehat{\theta}-\theta_{0}\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\theta},3)
with probability at least
1-\delta
when
\zeta=A_{3}A_{\varphi}\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}\log(d/\delta)}{n}}
. By Lemma
E.4
, whenever
d\geq s_{\theta}+2
, there exists a discrete set
M_{d,\theta}\subset B_{d}(0,1)
with cardinality
|M_{d,\theta}|\leq\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{5ed}{s_{\theta}}\right)^{s_{\theta}}
such that
\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\theta},3)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\subset 10\cdot\mathrm{conv}(M_{d,\theta})
and
\left|\left|u\right|\right|_{0}\leq s_{\theta}
for all
u\in M_{d,\theta}
. For any
t>0
, we bound the probability

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\left(\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\theta},3)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\leq}}n\cdot\mathrm{P}\left(\sup_{v\in 10\cdot\mathrm{conv}(M_{d,\theta})}\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}n\cdot\mathrm{P}\left(\sup_{v\in 10M_{d,\theta}}\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{\leq}}n|M_{d,\theta}|\sup_{v\in 10M_{d,\theta}}\mathrm{P}\left(\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iv)}}}{{\leq}}\frac{3n}{2}\left(\frac{5ed}{s_{\theta}}\right)^{s_{\theta}}\cdot 2\exp\left(-\frac{A_{0}t^{2}}{\phi_{s_{\theta}}}\right)

for some absolute constant
A_{0}>0
, where we apply the union bound in inequalities (i) and (iii), utilize Lemma
E.5
that the supremum of a convex function on
\mathrm{conv}(M_{d,\theta})
is always attained at its vertices which are contained in
M_{d,\theta}
to obtain the equality (ii), and leverage the sub-Gaussian bound under Assumption
2
to obtain (iv). It implies that

\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{\theta},3)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|\lesssim\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\theta}}\left[\log\left(\frac{n}{\delta}\right)+s_{\theta}\log\left(\frac{ed}{s_{\theta}}\right)\right]}

with probability at least
1-\delta
. By Lemma
E.2
, we also know that

\left|\left|\widehat{\theta}-\theta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}\lesssim\frac{A_{\varphi}}{\rho}\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}s_{\theta}\log(d/\delta)}{n}}

with probability at least
1-\delta
. Thus, we proceed (
56
) with Lemma
E.2
as:

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim L_{\varphi}\sqrt{\phi_{s_{\theta}}\left[\log\left(\frac{n}{\delta}\right)+s_{\theta}\log\left(\frac{ed}{s_{\theta}}\right)\right]}\cdot\left|\left|\widehat{\theta}-\theta_{0}\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{L_{\varphi}A_{\varphi}}{\rho}\sqrt{\frac{\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\theta}}s_{\theta}\log(d/\delta)}{n}\left[\log\left(\frac{n}{\delta}\right)+s_{\theta}\log\left(\frac{ed}{s_{\theta}}\right)\right]}

for all
i=1,...,n
with probability at least
1-\delta
. The result follows.
∎

E.9
Auxiliary Results

Recall that a cone of dominant coordinates is defined as:

\mathcal{C}_{q}(S,\vartheta)=\left\{v\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:\left|\left|v_{S^{c}}\right|\right|_{q}\leq\vartheta\left|\left|v_{S}\right|\right|_{q}\right\},

where
\vartheta\in[0,\infty]
. When
\vartheta=\infty
,
\mathcal{C}_{q}(S,\infty)=\mathbb{R}^{d}
; when
\vartheta=0
,
\mathcal{C}_{q}(S,0)
reduces to the linear subspace with
|S|
-sparse vectors whose nonzero entries are indexed by
S
in
\mathbb{R}^{d}
. The following lemma sheds light on the geometric properties of
\mathcal{C}_{q}(S,\vartheta)
.

Lemma E.4

(Size of cones of dominated coordinates with respect to the norm
\left|\left|\cdot\right|\right|_{q}
)
.

For any
\vartheta\in[0,\infty]
and
S\subseteq\{1,...,d\}
, we denote the cardinality of
S
by
s=|S|
. If
d\geq s+2
, then there exists a set
M_{d}\subset B_{d}(0,1)
such that
\left|\left|m\right|\right|_{0}\leq s
for all
m\in M_{d}
and

\mathcal{C}_{q}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\subset 2(2+\vartheta)\mathrm{conv}(M_{d})\quad\text{ with }\quad|M_{d}|\leq\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{5ed}{s}\right)^{s},

where
|M_{d}|
is the cardinality of
M_{d}
and
q\geq 1
.

Proof of Lemma
E.4
.

The main part of the proof follows from Lemma 7.1 in
Koltchinskii 2011
with generalization to the norm
\left|\left|\cdot\right|\right|_{q},q\geq 1
. It yields that

|M_{d}|\leq 5^{s}\sum_{k=0}^{s}\binom{d}{k}\leq 5^{s}\cdot\frac{3d^{s}}{2s!}\leq\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{5ed}{s}\right)^{s},

where the second inequality follows from Proposition 3.6.4 in
Giné and Nickl 2021
when
d\geq s+2
, and the nonasymptotic Stirling’s formula is also applied in the third inequality; see Page 52 in
Feller 1968
. Here, the factor
5^{s}
comes from the covering number of the unit ball; see Corollary 4.2.13 in
Vershynin 2018
.
∎

Lemma E.5

(Maxima of biconvex functions)
.

Let
f:\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}\to\mathbb{R}
be a biconvex function,
i.e.
, for any fixed
x\in\mathcal{X},y\in\mathcal{Y}
, the functions
f(\cdot,y),f(x,\cdot)
are convex. Then,

\sup_{x\in\mathrm{conv}(\mathcal{X})}\sup_{y\in\mathrm{conv}(\mathcal{Y})}f(x,y)=\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}}\sup_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}f(x,y).

Moreover, the identity still holds if we replace
f
with
|f|
.

Proof of Lemma
E.5
.

The proof is adopted from Lemma 1 in
Geng and Huang 2000
; see also Section 3.3 in
Gorski et al. 2007
. For any
x\in\mathrm{conv}(\mathcal{X})
, there exists an integer
k
and
\lambda_{1},...,\lambda_{k}\geq 0
with
\sum_{i=1}^{k}\lambda_{i}=1
such that
x=\sum_{i=1}^{k}\lambda_{i}x_{i}
for some
x_{1},...,x_{k}\in\mathcal{X}
. Similarly, for any
y\in\mathrm{conv}(\mathcal{Y})
, there exists an integer
m
and
\mu_{1},...,\mu_{m}\geq 0
with
\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mu_{j}=1
such that
y=\sum_{j=1}^{m}\mu_{j}y_{j}
for some
y_{1},...,y_{m}\in\mathcal{Y}
. Thus, by the biconvexity of
f
and Jensen’s inequality, we have that, for any
x\in\mathrm{conv}(\mathcal{X}),y\in\mathrm{conv}(\mathcal{Y})
,

f(x,y)\leq\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\lambda_{i}\mu_{j}f(x_{i},y_{j})\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\lambda_{i}\mu_{j}\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}}\sup_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}f(x,y)=\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}}\sup_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}f(x,y).

It implies that
\sup_{x\in\mathrm{conv}(\mathcal{X})}\sup_{y\in\mathrm{conv}(\mathcal{Y})}f(x,y)\leq\sup_{x\in\mathcal{X}}\sup_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}f(x,y)
. The reverse inequality holds trivially because
\mathcal{X}\subseteq\mathrm{conv}(\mathcal{X}),\mathcal{Y}\subseteq\mathrm{conv}(\mathcal{Y})
. The same arguments still apply when we replace
f
with
|f|
. The proof is completed.
∎

Lemma E.6
.

Let
a\geq\sqrt{e}
and
b,K>0
be some constants. If a random variable
Z
is positive almost surely with the tail bound
\mathrm{P}(Z>t)\leq a\exp\left(-b\min\left\{\frac{t^{2}}{K^{2}},\frac{t}{K}\right\}\right)
for all
t>0
, then

\mathrm{E}(Z)\leq 2K\sqrt{\frac{\log a}{b}}+\frac{K\log a}{b}+\frac{K}{b}.

Proof of Lemma
E.6
.

By Lemma 1.2.1 in
Vershynin 2018
, we can bound
\mathrm{E}(Z)
as:

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}(Z)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{P}(Z>t)\,dt

\displaystyle\leq\inf_{u_{1}>0}\left[\int_{0}^{u_{1}}dt+\int_{u_{1}}^{\infty}a\exp\left(-\frac{bt^{2}}{K^{2}}\right)dt\right]+\inf_{u_{2}>0}\left[\int_{0}^{u_{2}}dt+\int_{u_{2}}^{\infty}a\exp\left(-\frac{bt}{K}\right)dt\right]

\displaystyle:=\inf_{u_{1}>0}f(u_{1})+\inf_{u_{2}>0}g(u_{2}),

where
u_{1},u_{2}>0
are two (nonrandom) variables to be optimized,
f(u_{1})=u_{1}+\int_{u_{1}}^{\infty}a\exp\left(-\frac{bt^{2}}{K^{2}}\right)dt
, and
g(u_{2})=u_{2}+\frac{aK}{b}\exp\left(-\frac{bu_{2}}{K}\right)
. By calculating
f^{\prime}(u_{1})
and
g^{\prime}(u_{2})
, we know that
f
attains its minimum at
u_{1}^{*}=K\sqrt{\frac{\log a}{b}}
and
g
has its minimum at
u_{2}^{*}=\frac{K\log a}{b}
. Thus,

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}(Z)

\displaystyle\leq K\sqrt{\frac{\log a}{b}}+\int_{u_{1}^{*}}^{\infty}\frac{at}{u_{1}^{*}}\exp\left(-\frac{bt^{2}}{K^{2}}\right)dt+\frac{K\log a}{b}+\frac{K}{b}

\displaystyle=K\sqrt{\frac{\log a}{b}}+\frac{aK^{2}}{2u_{1}^{*}b}\exp\left(-\frac{b\left(u_{1}^{*}\right)^{2}}{K^{2}}\right)+\frac{K\log a}{b}+\frac{K}{b}

\displaystyle=K\sqrt{\frac{\log a}{b}}+\frac{K}{2\sqrt{b\log a}}+\frac{K\log a}{b}+\frac{K}{b}

\displaystyle=K\sqrt{\frac{\log a}{b}}\left(1+\frac{1}{2\log a}\right)+\frac{K\log a}{b}+\frac{K}{b}

\displaystyle\leq 2K\sqrt{\frac{\log a}{b}}+\frac{K\log a}{b}+\frac{K}{b},

where we leverage the fact that
a\geq\sqrt{e}
to obtain the last inequality. The result follows.
∎

Lemma E.7
.

Let
\vartheta\in[0,\infty]
and
\delta\in(0,1)
be arbitrary. Suppose that Assumption
2
holds. Let
S\subset\{1,...,d\}
with
|S|=s
. If
d\geq s+2
, then

\mathrm{E}\left[\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\Big\}\right|\right]\lesssim(2+\vartheta)^{2}\phi_{s}\left[\sqrt{\frac{s\log(ed/s)}{n}}+\frac{s\log(ed/s)}{n}\right]

and with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\Big\}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+\vartheta)^{2}\phi_{s}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}\right].

In addition, these bounds also apply to

\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big\{R_{i}(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[R_{i}(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\Big\}\right|

for the complete-case analysis and

\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\frac{R_{i}}{\pi_{i}}(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{R_{i}}{\pi_{i}}(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|

for the IPW analysis with the true propensity scores
\pi_{i}=\mathrm{P}(R_{i}=1|X_{i}),i=1,...,n
under Assumption
1
. Finally, the above asymptotic upper bound remains almost identical for

\displaystyle\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{1},\vartheta_{2})\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S_{2},\vartheta_{1})\cap\mathbb{S}^{d-1}\end{subarray}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big\{(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)\right]\Big\}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+\vartheta_{1})(2+\vartheta_{2})\phi_{s}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{1}\log(ed/s_{1})+s_{2}\log(ed/s_{2})}{n}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s_{1}\log(ed/s_{1})+s_{2}\log(ed/s_{2})}{n}\right]

with
|S_{1}|=s_{1}
and
|S_{2}|=s_{2}
.

Proof of Lemma
E.7
.

By Lemma
E.4
, whenever
d\geq s+2
, there exists a discrete set
M_{d}\subset B_{d}(0,1)
with cardinality
|M_{d}|\leq\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{5ed}{s}\right)^{s}
such that
\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\subset 2(2+\vartheta)\,\mathrm{conv}(M_{d})
and
\left|\left|u\right|\right|_{0}\leq s
for all
u\in M_{d}
. For any
t>0
, we consider the probability

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathrm{P}\left(\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\Big\}\right|>t\right)\\
&\leq\mathrm{P}\left(\sup_{u,v\in 2(2+\vartheta)\mathrm{conv}(M_{d})}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}v^{T}\left[X_{i}X_{i}^{T}-\mathrm{E}\left(X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right)\right]u\right|>t\right).\end{split}

(57)

Notice that
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}v^{T}\left[X_{i}X_{i}^{T}-\mathrm{E}\left(X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right)\right]u
is an affine function of
u
(or
v
) for any fixed
v\in 2(2+\vartheta)M_{d}
(or
u\in 2(2+\vartheta)M_{d}
) and is thus biconvex. By Lemma
E.5
, we know that the supremum of a biconvex function on
\mathrm{conv}(M_{d})
is always attained at its vertices, which are contained in
M_{d}
. Hence, we can proceed (
57
) via the union bound as:

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\left(\sup_{u,v\in 2(2+\vartheta)M_{d}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big\{(X_{i}^{T}v)(X_{i}^{T}u)-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)(X_{i}^{T}u)\right]\Big\}\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq|M_{d}|^{2}\sup_{u,v\in 2(2+\vartheta)M_{d}}\mathrm{P}\left(\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big\{(X_{i}^{T}v)(X_{i}^{T}u)-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)(X_{i}^{T}u)\right]\Big\}\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{9}{4}\left(\frac{5ed}{s}\right)^{2s}\sup_{u,v\in 2(2+\vartheta)M_{d}}\mathrm{P}\left(\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big\{(X_{i}^{T}v)(X_{i}^{T}u)-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)(X_{i}^{T}u)\right]\Big\}\right|>t\right).

Now, we note that under Assumption
2
,
(X_{i}^{T}v)(X_{i}^{T}u)-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)(X_{i}^{T}u)\right],i=1,...,n
are independent, centered, and sub-exponential random variables for all
u,v\in 2(2+\vartheta)M_{d}
, and

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\left|v^{T}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}u-v^{T}\mathrm{E}\left(X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right)u\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left(\left|\left|v^{T}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}u\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}+\left|\left|v^{T}\mathrm{E}\left(X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right)u\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left(\left|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\left|\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}+\left|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\left|\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+\vartheta)^{2}\phi_{s},

where we use Lemma
E.13
in the second (asymptotic) inequality. Therefore, by Bernstein’s inequality (Corollary 2.8.3 in
Vershynin 2018
), we conclude that there exists an absolute constant
A_{0}>0
such that for any
t>0
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\left(\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\Big\}\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{9}{2}\left(\frac{5ed}{s}\right)^{2s}\exp\left(-nA_{0}\min\left\{\frac{t^{2}}{(2+\vartheta)^{4}\phi_{s}^{2}},\,\frac{t}{(2+\vartheta)^{2}\phi_{s}}\right\}\right).

Hence, for any
\delta\in(0,1)
, we have that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\Big\}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+\vartheta)^{2}\phi_{s}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}\right].

Finally, we bound the expectation of the above supremum by Lemma
E.6
as:

\displaystyle\mathrm{E}\left[\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\Big\}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+\vartheta)^{2}\phi_{s}\left[\sqrt{\frac{s\log(ed/s)}{n}}+\frac{s\log(ed/s)}{n}\right],

where we set
a=\frac{9}{2}\left(\frac{5ed}{s}\right)^{2s}\geq\sqrt{e}
,
b=nC_{0}
, and
K=(2+\vartheta)^{2}\phi_{s}
in Lemma
E.6
.

As for the complete-case analysis, one can follow the same proof and notice that
R_{i}X_{i},i=1,...,n
are also sub-Gaussian under Assumption
2
and for all
u,v\in 2(2+\vartheta)M_{d}
,

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\left|R_{i}(X_{i}^{T}v)(X_{i}^{T}u)-\mathrm{E}\left[R_{i}(X_{i}^{T}v)(X_{i}^{T}u)\right]\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left(\left|\left|v^{T}X_{i}R_{i}X_{i}^{T}u\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}+\left|\left|v^{T}\mathrm{E}\left(X_{i}R_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right)u\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left(\left|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\left|\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}+\left|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\left|\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+\vartheta)^{2}\phi_{s}.

As for the IPW analysis under the true propensity scores
\pi_{i}\equiv\pi(X_{i})=\mathrm{P}(R_{i}=1|X_{i}),i=1,...,n
, we can also calculate that

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|\left|\frac{R_{i}}{\pi(X_{i})}(X_{i}^{T}v)(X_{i}^{T}u)-\mathrm{E}\left[\frac{R_{i}}{\pi(X_{i})}(X_{i}^{T}v)(X_{i}^{T}u)\right]\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left(\left|\left|v^{T}X_{i}\cdot\frac{R_{i}}{\pi(X_{i})}\cdot X_{i}^{T}u\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}+\left|\left|v^{T}\mathrm{E}\left(X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\right)u\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left(\left|\left|\frac{R_{i}}{\sqrt{\pi(X_{i})}}\cdot X_{i}^{T}v\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\left|\left|\frac{R_{i}}{\sqrt{\pi(X_{i})}}\cdot X_{i}^{T}u\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}+\left|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\left|\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left[\mathrm{E}\left(v^{T}X_{i}\cdot\frac{R_{i}}{\pi(X_{i})}\cdot X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]\left[\mathrm{E}\left(u^{T}X_{i}\cdot\frac{R_{i}}{\pi(X_{i})}\cdot X_{i}^{T}u\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]+\left[\mathrm{E}\left(v^{T}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]\left[\mathrm{E}\left(u^{T}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}u\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\left[\mathrm{E}\left(v^{T}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}v\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]\left[\mathrm{E}\left(u^{T}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}u\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+\vartheta)^{2}\phi_{s},

where we leverage the fact that propensity scores
\pi(X_{i}),i=1,...,n
are nonnegative and apply the law of total expectation.
∎

Lemma E.8
.

Under Definition
1
and Assumption
6
, we have that

\ell_{0}(x)\in\mathcal{C}_{2}\left(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell}\right)=\left\{v\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:\left|\left|v_{S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\right|\right|_{2}\leq\vartheta_{\ell}\left|\left|v_{S_{\ell}(x)}\right|\right|_{2}\right\}\quad\text{ with }\quad\vartheta_{\ell}=\frac{r_{\ell}}{\sqrt{1-r_{\ell}^{2}}}

for any
r_{\ell}\in[0,1]
. Moreover, if
0\leq r_{\ell}\leq\frac{1}{2}
, then

\left|\left|M^{T}\left(\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right)\right|\right|_{2}\leq\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap B_{d}\left(0,10r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\right)}\left|\left|M^{T}v\right|\right|_{2}

for any matrix
M\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times k}
.

Lemma
E.8
.

The proof is modified from Lemma 24 in
Giessing and Wang 2023
. By Assumption
6
, we know that

\displaystyle\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\in S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\left[\ell_{0}(x)\right]_{k}^{2}\leq r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}=r_{\ell}^{2}\left(\sum_{k\in S_{\ell}(x)}\left[\ell_{0}(x)\right]_{k}^{2}+\sum_{k\in S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\left[\ell_{0}(x)\right]_{k}^{2}\right).

Some rearrangements show that
\sum_{k\in S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\left[\ell_{0}(x)\right]_{k}^{2}\leq\frac{r_{\ell}^{2}}{1-r_{\ell}^{2}}\sum_{k\in S_{\ell}(x)}\left[\ell_{0}(x)\right]_{k}^{2}
, that is,

\left|\left|\left[\ell_{0}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\right|\right|_{2}\leq\frac{r_{\ell}}{\sqrt{1-r_{\ell}^{2}}}\left|\left|\left[\ell_{0}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)}\right|\right|_{2}.

Let
S_{\ell}:=S_{\ell}(x)
in the sequel. We know that

\ell_{0}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)=\left(0_{S_{\ell}},\left[\ell_{0}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}^{c}}\right)\quad\text{ with }\quad\ell_{0}(x)\in\mathcal{C}_{2}\left(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell}\right).

In addition, for any
v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}\left(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell}\right)
, we have that

u=t\left(v_{S_{\ell}},0_{S_{\ell}^{c}}\right)+\left(0_{S_{\ell}},v_{S_{\ell}^{c}}\right)\in\mathcal{C}_{2}\left(S_{\ell}(x),1\right)\quad\text{ when }\quad|t|\geq\vartheta_{\ell}=\frac{r_{\ell}}{\sqrt{1-r_{\ell}^{2}}},

because

\left|\left|u_{S_{\ell}^{c}}\right|\right|_{2}=\left|\left|v_{S_{\ell}^{c}}\right|\right|_{2}\leq\vartheta_{\ell}\left|\left|v_{S_{\ell}}\right|\right|_{2}\leq|t|\cdot\left|\left|v_{S_{\ell}}\right|\right|_{2}=\left|\left|u_{S_{\ell}}\right|\right|_{2}.

As a result, we obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+r_{\ell}}\cdot\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\frac{1}{1-r_{\ell}}\cdot\ell_{0}(x)

\displaystyle=-\frac{2r_{\ell}}{1-r_{\ell}^{2}}\cdot\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\frac{1}{1-r_{\ell}}\left[\ell_{0}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{2r_{\ell}}{1-r_{\ell}^{2}}\left(\left[\ell_{0}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}},0_{S_{\ell}^{c}}\right)-\frac{1}{1-r_{\ell}}\left(0_{S_{\ell}},\left[\ell_{0}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}^{c}}\right)

\displaystyle\in\mathcal{C}_{2}\left(S_{\ell}(x),1\right),

since
\frac{2r_{\ell}}{1-r_{\ell}^{2}}(1-r_{\ell})\geq\frac{r_{\ell}}{\sqrt{1-r_{\ell}^{2}}}
when
0\leq r_{\ell}\leq\frac{3}{5}
. Similarly,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+2r_{\ell}}\cdot\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)

\displaystyle=-\frac{2r_{\ell}}{1+2r_{\ell}}\cdot\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\left[\ell_{0}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{2r_{\ell}}{1+2r_{\ell}}\left(\left[\ell_{0}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}},0_{S_{\ell}^{c}}\right)-\left(0_{S_{\ell}},\left[\ell_{0}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}^{c}}\right)

\displaystyle\in\mathcal{C}_{2}\left(S_{\ell}(x),1\right),

since
\frac{2r_{\ell}}{1+2r_{\ell}}\geq\frac{r_{\ell}}{\sqrt{1-r_{\ell}^{2}}}
when
0\leq r_{\ell}\leq\frac{1}{2}
. Now, we notice that

\left|\left|M^{T}\left[\ell_{0}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]\right|\right|_{2}^{2}=\left|\left|M^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}+\left|\left|M^{T}\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}-2\left[M^{T}\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{T}\left[M^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right].

(58)

Case I:
If
\widetilde{\ell}(x)^{T}MM^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\leq 0
, then the right-hand side of (
58
) can be upper bounded by

\displaystyle\left|\left|M^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}+\left|\left|M^{T}\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}-2\left[M^{T}\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{T}\left[M^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\leq}}(1+r_{\ell})^{2}\left[\frac{1}{(1+r_{\ell})^{2}}\left|\left|M^{T}\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{(1-r_{\ell})^{2}}\left|\left|M^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}-\frac{2}{1-r_{\ell}^{2}}\cdot\widetilde{\ell}(x)^{T}MM^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]

\displaystyle=(1+r_{\ell})^{2}\left[\frac{1}{1+r_{\ell}}\cdot\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\frac{1}{1-r_{\ell}}\cdot\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{T}MM^{T}\left[\frac{1}{1+r_{\ell}}\cdot\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\frac{1}{1-r_{\ell}}\cdot\ell_{0}(x)\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{\leq}}(1+r_{\ell})^{2}\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap B_{d}\left(0,5r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\right)}\left|\left|M^{T}v\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap B_{d}\left(0,10r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\right)}\left|\left|M^{T}v\right|\right|_{2}^{2},

where the inequality (i) follows from the facts that
(1+r_{\ell})^{2}\geq 1,(1+r_{\ell})^{2}\geq 1-r_{\ell}^{2}
, and we compute that

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{1}{1+r_{\ell}}\cdot\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\frac{1}{1-r_{\ell}}\cdot\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left|\left|-\frac{2r_{\ell}}{1-r_{\ell}^{2}}\cdot\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\frac{1}{1-r_{\ell}}\left[\ell_{0}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq r_{\ell}\left(\frac{2}{1-r_{\ell}^{2}}+\frac{1}{1-r_{\ell}}\right)\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 5r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

for any
0\leq r_{\ell}\leq\frac{1}{2}
to obtain the inequality (ii).

Case II:
If
\widetilde{\ell}(x)^{T}MM^{T}\ell_{0}(x)>0
, then the right-hand side of (
58
) can be upper bounded by

\displaystyle\left|\left|M^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}+\left|\left|M^{T}\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}-2\left[M^{T}\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{T}\left[M^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{\leq}}(1+2r_{\ell})^{2}\left[\frac{1}{(1+2r_{\ell})^{2}}\left|\left|M^{T}\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}+\left|\left|M^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}-\frac{2}{1+2r_{\ell}}\cdot\widetilde{\ell}(x)^{T}MM^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]

\displaystyle=(1+2r_{\ell})^{2}\left[\frac{1}{1+2r_{\ell}}\cdot\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{T}MM^{T}\left[\frac{1}{1+2r_{\ell}}\cdot\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iv)}}}{{\leq}}(1+2r_{\ell})^{2}\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap B_{d}\left(0,3r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\right)}\left|\left|M^{T}v\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap B_{d}\left(0,6r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\right)}\left|\left|M^{T}v\right|\right|_{2}^{2},

where the inequality (iii) follows from

\displaystyle\left[(1+2r_{\ell})^{2}-1\right]\left|\left|M^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=(4r_{\ell}^{2}+4r_{\ell})\left|\left|M^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq 4r_{\ell}\left[M^{T}\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{T}\left[M^{T}\ell_{0}(x)\right]

by Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, and we calculate that

\displaystyle\left|\left|\frac{1}{1+2r_{\ell}}\cdot\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left|\left|-\frac{2r_{\ell}}{1+r_{\ell}}\cdot\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\left[\ell_{0}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq r_{\ell}\left(\frac{2}{1+2r_{\ell}}+1\right)\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}

to obtain the inequality (iv). Therefore, we combine the above two cases with (
58
) to conclude that

\left|\left|M^{T}\left(\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right)\right|\right|_{2}\leq\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(S_{\ell}(x),1)\cap B_{d}\left(0,10r_{\ell}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}\right)}\left|\left|M^{T}v\right|\right|_{2}

for any matrix
M\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times k}
and
0\leq r_{\ell}\leq\frac{1}{2}
.
∎

Lemma E.9

(Restricted cone and cross polytope property)
.

Let
a_{0}>1
be some absolute constant. Under Assumptions
4
and
6
, if

\left|\left|\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\cdot\ell_{0}(x)^{T}X_{i}X_{i}+x\right|\right|_{\infty}\leq\frac{\gamma}{a_{0}n}\quad\text{ and }\quad\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}\left(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell}\right)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left[X_{i}^{T}v\right]^{2}\right|\leq\frac{\nu}{a_{0}n}

(59)

with
\vartheta_{\ell}=\frac{r_{\ell}}{\sqrt{1-r_{\ell}^{2}}}
, then

\sum_{k\in S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\left|\widehat{\ell}_{k}(x)\right|\leq\frac{4r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{(a_{0}-1)\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{\nu}{\gamma}+\left(\frac{a_{0}+1}{a_{0}-1}\right)\sum_{k\in S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\left|\widehat{\ell}_{k}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}_{k}(x)\right|

so that

\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\in\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),2\left(\frac{a_{0}+1}{a_{0}-1}\right)\right)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}\left(0,\,\frac{(a_{0}^{2}+1)r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{(a_{0}-1)\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{\nu}{\gamma}\right),

where
B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r)=\left\{v\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:\left|\left|v\right|\right|_{1}\leq r\right\}
.

Remark E.2
.

The same results hold true when the estimated propensity scores
\widehat{\pi}_{i},i=1,...,n
are replaced by the true ones
\pi_{i},i=1,...,n
.

Proof of Lemma
E.9
.

The proof is modified from the original arguments of Lemma 24 in
Giessing and Wang 2023
. Since
\widehat{\ell}(x)
minimizes the objective function of the dual problem (
8
), we know that

\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left[X_{i}^{T}\widehat{\ell}(x)\right]^{2}+x^{T}\widehat{\ell}(x)+\frac{\gamma}{n}\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}\leq\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left[X_{i}^{T}\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]^{2}+x^{T}\widetilde{\ell}(x)+\frac{\gamma}{n}\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}.

Taking Taylor’s expansion at
\ell_{0}(x)
on both sides of the above inequality gives us that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{T}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)+x\right]^{T}\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]+\frac{\gamma}{n}\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{T}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)+x\right]^{T}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]+\frac{\gamma}{n}\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}.

Some rearrangements show that

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{T}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\widehat{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]^{T}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\ell_{0}(x)\right]+\left[\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}X_{i}X_{i}^{T}\ell_{0}(x)+x\right]^{T}\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)-\widehat{\ell}(x)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\gamma}{n}\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}-\frac{\gamma}{n}\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\leq}}\frac{r_{\ell}^{2}\nu}{4a_{0}n}\left|\left|\ell_{0}(x)\right|\right|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\gamma}{a_{0}n}\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}+\frac{\gamma}{n}\left|\left|\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}-\frac{\gamma}{n}\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{\leq}}\frac{r_{\ell}^{2}\nu\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{a_{0}\cdot n\cdot\kappa_{R}^{4}}+\frac{\gamma}{a_{0}n}\left[\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)}\right|\right|_{1}+\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\right|\right|_{1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\gamma}{n}\left|\left|\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)}\right|\right|_{1}-\frac{\gamma}{n}\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)}+\left[\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)}\right|\right|_{1}-\frac{\gamma}{n}\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\right|\right|_{1}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{\leq}}\frac{r_{\ell}^{2}\nu\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{a_{0}\cdot n\cdot\kappa_{R}^{4}}+\frac{\gamma}{a_{0}n}\left[\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)}\right|\right|_{1}+\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\right|\right|_{1}\right]+\frac{\gamma}{n}\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)}\right|\right|_{1}-\frac{\gamma}{n}\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\right|\right|_{1},

where we utilize our condition (
59
) to obtain the inequality (i), denote
\widehat{\Delta}(x)=\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)
and apply Assumption
4
in the inequality (ii), and leverage the reverse triangle’s inequality in (iii). Hence,

0\leq\frac{r_{\ell}^{2}\nu\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{a_{0}\cdot n\cdot\kappa_{R}^{4}}+\frac{(1+a_{0})\gamma}{a_{0}\cdot n}\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)}\right|\right|_{1}-\frac{(a_{0}-1)\gamma}{a_{0}\cdot n}\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\right|\right|_{1},

which implies that

\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\right|\right|_{1}\leq\frac{r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{(a_{0}-1)\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{\nu}{\gamma}+\left(\frac{a_{0}+1}{a_{0}-1}\right)\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)}\right|\right|_{1}.

On the one hand, if
\frac{r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{(a_{0}-1)\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{\nu}{\gamma}\leq\left(\frac{a_{0}+1}{a_{0}-1}\right)\sum_{k\in S_{\ell}(x)}\left|\widehat{\ell}_{k}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}_{k}(x)\right|
, then

\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)^{c}}\right|\right|_{1}\leq 2\left(\frac{a_{0}+1}{a_{0}-1}\right)\left|\left|\left[\widehat{\Delta}(x)\right]_{S_{\ell}(x)}\right|\right|_{1}\quad\text{ so that }\quad\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\in\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),2\left(\frac{a_{0}+1}{a_{0}-1}\right)\right).

On the other hand, if
\frac{r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{(a_{0}-1)\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{\nu}{\gamma}>\left(\frac{a_{0}+1}{a_{0}-1}\right)\sum_{k\in S_{\ell}(x)}\left|\widehat{\ell}_{k}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}_{k}(x)\right|
, then

\left|\left|\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\right|\right|_{1}\leq\frac{(a_{0}^{2}+1)r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{(a_{0}-1)\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{\nu}{\gamma}.

Thus, we conclude that

\widehat{\ell}(x)-\widetilde{\ell}(x)\in\mathcal{C}_{1}\left(S_{\ell}(x),2\left(\frac{a_{0}+1}{a_{0}-1}\right)\right)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}\left(0,\,\frac{(a_{0}^{2}+1)r_{\ell}^{2}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}^{2}}{(a_{0}-1)\kappa_{R}^{4}}\cdot\frac{\nu}{\gamma}\right).

The proof is completed.
∎

Lemma E.10
.

Let
\vartheta,r_{0},r_{1}\in(0,\infty)
and
\delta\in(0,1)
be arbitrary. Suppose that Assumption
2
holds. Let
S,\widetilde{S}\subset\{1,...,d\}
with
|S|=s
and
|\widetilde{S}|=\widetilde{s}
. If
d\geq s+2
, then with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in\left[\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\right]\bigcap B_{d}(0,r_{0})}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{0}^{2}(2+\vartheta)^{2}\phi_{s}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}\right]+r_{1}^{2}\phi_{1}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}}+\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}\right].

In particular, when
r_{0}\geq r_{1}
, we further have that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in\left[\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\right]\bigcap B_{d}(0,r_{0})}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{0}^{2}(2+\vartheta)^{2}\phi_{s}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}\right].

Similarly, if
d\geq\max\{s,\widetilde{s}\}+2
, then with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\\
v\in\left[\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\right]\bigcap B_{d}(0,r_{0})\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left[r_{0}(2+\vartheta)^{2}+r_{1}\right]\sqrt{\phi_{\widetilde{s}}\cdot\phi_{1}}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}+1\right].

Remark E.3
.

Notice that the structure of
\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})
is no more complicated than the cone of dominated coordinates
\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)
. The supremum of a (bi)convex function over the
L_{1}
-ball
B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})
is always attained at its vertex set
r_{1}\cdot\{e_{1},...,e_{d}\}
with
e_{1},...,e_{d}
being the standard basis vectors in
\mathbb{R}^{d}
, leading to a simpler discretization than the one for
\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)
in Lemma
E.4
. Hence, the probabilistic bounds for taking the supremum over
\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})
in Lemma
E.10
are dominated by the probabilistic bounds for taking the supremum over a cone of dominant coordinates
\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)
in Lemma
E.7
.

Proof of Lemma
E.10
.

We first notice that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{v\in\left[\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\right]\bigcap B_{d}(0,r_{0})}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|\\
&\leq\underbrace{\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,r_{0})}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|}_{\textbf{Term I}}\\
&\quad+\underbrace{\sup_{v\in B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\cap B_{d}(0,r_{0})}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|}_{\textbf{Term II}}.\end{split}

(60)

\bullet

Term I:
We know from Lemma
E.7
that

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,r_{0})}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\leq r_{0}^{2}\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{0}^{2}(2+\vartheta)^{2}\phi_{s}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}\right].

\bullet

Term II:
Note that

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\cap B_{d}(0,r_{0})}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle=\sup_{u,v\in B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\cap B_{d}(0,r_{0})}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{u,v\in B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)\right]\right\}\right|.

(61)

Since
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)\right]\right\}
is a biconvex function with respect to
u,v
, we know from Lemma
E.5
that the supremum is attained at the vertex set

\left\{x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{0}=1,\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{1}=\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}=r_{1}\right\}=r_{1}\cdot\left\{e_{1},...,e_{d}\right\}

of the
L_{1}
-ball
B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})
, where
e_{1},...,e_{d}
are the standard basis vectors in
\mathbb{R}^{d}
. We further upper bound (
E.9
) as:

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\leq r_{1}^{2}\sup_{u,v\in\{e_{1},...,e_{d}\}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}u)(X_{i}^{T}v)\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle=r_{1}^{2}\max_{1\leq j,k\leq d}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[X_{ij}X_{ik}-\mathrm{E}\left(X_{ij}X_{ik}\right)\right]\right|.

Under Assumption
2
, we note that
X_{ij}X_{ik}-\mathrm{E}\left(X_{ij}X_{ik}\right),i=1,...,n
are independent, centered, and sub-exponential random variables for all
j,k=1,...,d
, and

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j,k\leq d}\left|\left|X_{ij}X_{ik}-\mathrm{E}\left(X_{ij}X_{ik}\right)\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq j,k\leq d}\left[\left|\left|X_{ij}X_{ik}\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}+\left|\left|\mathrm{E}\left(X_{ij}X_{ik}\right)\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{1\leq j,k\leq d}\left|\left|X_{ij}\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\left|\left|X_{ik}\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\phi_{1}.

Thus, by Bernstein’s inequality (Corollary 2.8.3 in
Vershynin 2018
) and the union bound, we obtain that there exists an absolute constant
A_{0}>0
such that for any
t>0
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\left(\max_{1\leq j,k\leq d}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[X_{ij}X_{ik}-\mathrm{E}\left(X_{ij}X_{ik}\right)\right]\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2d^{2}\exp\left(-nA_{0}\min\left\{\frac{t^{2}}{\phi_{1}^{2}},\frac{t}{\phi_{1}}\right\}\right).

Hence, for any
\delta\in(0,1)
, we have that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\max_{1\leq j,k\leq d}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[X_{ij}X_{ik}-\mathrm{E}\left(X_{ij}X_{ik}\right)\right]\right|\lesssim\phi_{1}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}}+\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}\right).

Plugging the bounds for
Term I
and
Term II
into (
60
), we conclude that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in\left[\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\right]\bigcap B_{d}(0,r_{0})}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{0}^{2}(2+\vartheta)^{2}\phi_{s}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}\right]+r_{1}^{2}\phi_{1}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}}+\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}\right).

In particular, when
d\geq s+2
and
r_{0}\geq r_{1}
, we have that
\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta}\right)+s\log\left(\frac{ed}{s}\right)\geq\log\left(\frac{d}{\delta}\right)
and therefore, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in\left[\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\right]\bigcap B_{d}(0,r_{0})}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}-\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}v)^{2}\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{0}^{2}(2+\vartheta)^{2}\phi_{s}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}\right].

Similarly, we notice that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\\
v\in\left[\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\right]\bigcap B_{d}(0,r_{0})\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|\\
&\leq\underbrace{\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,r_{0})\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|}_{\textbf{Term III}}+\underbrace{\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\\
v\in B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\cap B_{d}(0,r_{0})\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|}_{\textbf{Term IV}}.\end{split}

(62)

\bullet

Term III:
We know from Lemma
E.7
that

\displaystyle\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,r_{0})\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|

\displaystyle\leq r_{0}\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\end{subarray}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|-\mathrm{E}\left(|X_{i}^{T}u||X_{i}^{T}v|\right)\right]\right|+r_{0}\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\\
v\in\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\end{subarray}}\mathrm{E}\left(|X_{i}^{T}u||X_{i}^{T}v|\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{0}(2+\vartheta)^{2}\sqrt{\phi_{s}\cdot\phi_{\widetilde{s}}}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}+1\right].

\bullet

Term IV:
Note that

\displaystyle\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\\
v\in B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\cap B_{d}(0,r_{0})\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\\
v\in B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|.

Since
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|
is a biconvex function with respect to
u,v
, we know from Lemma
E.5
that the supremum is attain at the vertex set

\left\{x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{0}=1,\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{1}=\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}=r_{1}\right\}=r_{1}\cdot\left\{e_{1},...,e_{d}\right\}

of the
L_{1}
-ball
B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})
, where
e_{1},...,e_{d}
are the standard basis vectors in
\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Hence,

\displaystyle\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\\
v\in B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\cap B_{d}(0,r_{0})\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\\
v\in B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|

\displaystyle\leq r_{1}\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\\
v\in\{e_{1},...,e_{d}\}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|

\displaystyle=r_{1}\sup_{u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\max_{1\leq k\leq d}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[|X_{i}^{T}u||X_{ik}|-\mathrm{E}\left(|X_{i}^{T}u||X_{ik}|\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+r_{1}\sup_{u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\max_{1\leq k\leq d}\mathrm{E}\left(|X_{i}^{T}u||X_{ik}|\right).

Again, under Assumption
2
,
|X_{i}^{T}u||X_{ik}|-\mathrm{E}\left(|X_{i}^{T}u||X_{ik}|\right),i=1,...,n
are independent, centered, and sub-exponential random variables for all
k=1,...,d
, and

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq k\leq d}\left|\left||X_{i}^{T}u||X_{ik}|-\mathrm{E}\left(|X_{i}^{T}u||X_{ik}|\right)\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq k\leq d}\left[\left|\left|(X_{i}^{T}u)X_{ik}\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}+\left|\left|\mathrm{E}\left[(X_{i}^{T}u)X_{ik}\right]\right|\right|_{\psi_{1}}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{1\leq k\leq d}\left|\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}\left|\left|X_{ik}\right|\right|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\phi_{\widetilde{s}}\cdot\phi_{1}}.

Thus, by Bernstein’s inequality, Lemma
E.4
, and the union bound, we obtain that there exists an absolute constant
A_{0}>0
such that for any
t>0
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}\left(\sup_{u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\max_{1\leq k\leq d}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[|X_{i}^{T}u||X_{ik}|-\mathrm{E}\left(|X_{i}^{T}u||X_{ik}|\right)\right]\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq 3d\left(\frac{5ed}{s}\right)^{s}\exp\left(-nA_{0}\min\left\{\frac{t^{2}}{\phi_{\widetilde{s}}\cdot\phi_{1}},\frac{t}{\sqrt{\phi_{\widetilde{s}}\cdot\phi_{1}}}\right\}\right).

Hence, for any
\delta\in(0,1)
, we have that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\max_{1\leq k\leq d}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[|X_{i}^{T}u||X_{ik}|-\mathrm{E}\left(|X_{i}^{T}u||X_{ik}|\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\phi_{\widetilde{s}}\cdot\phi_{1}}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}\right].

In addition, by Lemma
E.4
, we also have that

\displaystyle\sup_{u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\max_{1\leq k\leq d}\mathrm{E}\left(|X_{i}^{T}u||X_{ik}|\right)\lesssim\sqrt{\phi_{\widetilde{s}}\cdot\phi_{1}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\\
v\in B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\cap B_{d}(0,r_{0})\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim r_{1}\sqrt{\phi_{\widetilde{s}}\cdot\phi_{1}}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}+1\right].

Finally, plugging the bounds for
Term III
and
Term IV
into (
62
), we conclude that with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}u\in\mathcal{C}_{2}(\widetilde{S},\vartheta)\cap B_{d}(0,1)\\
v\in\left[\mathcal{C}_{1}(S,\vartheta)\bigcup B_{d}^{(1)}(0,r_{1})\right]\bigcap B_{d}(0,r_{0})\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|X_{i}^{T}u\right|\left|X_{i}^{T}v\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left[r_{0}(2+\vartheta)^{2}+r_{1}\right]\sqrt{\phi_{\widetilde{s}}\cdot\phi_{1}}\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta)+s\log(ed/s)}{n}+1\right].

The results follow.
∎

We know from (b) of
Theorem 4
that

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma}{a_{0}n}

\displaystyle\asymp r_{\gamma}(n,\delta)=\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}}}{\kappa_{R}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log(d/\delta)}{n}}+\frac{\sqrt{\phi_{1}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}}\left|\left|x\right|\right|_{2}}{\kappa_{R}^{2}}\cdot r_{\pi}

will satisfy the bound in (
59
) with probability at least
1-\delta
. The following lemma suggests a (probabilistic) lower bound for
\frac{\nu}{a_{0}n}
in (
59
) using the similar arguments. It is worth noting that the lower bound for
\frac{\gamma}{a_{0}n}
can converge to 0 as
n\to\infty
under certain conditions, while the lower bound for
\frac{\nu}{a_{0}n}
is asymptotically non-vanishing.

Lemma E.11
.

Let
\delta\in(0,1)
and
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
be a fixed covariate vector. Suppose that Assumption
2
holds. Then, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}\left(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell}\right)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\pi_{i}\left[X_{i}^{T}v\right]^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim(2+\vartheta_{\ell})^{2}\phi_{s_{\ell}(x)}\left[1+\sqrt{\frac{\log(\frac{1}{\delta})+s_{\ell}(x)\log\left(\frac{ed}{s_{\ell}(x)}\right)}{n}}+\frac{\log(\frac{1}{\delta})+s_{\ell}(x)\log\left(\frac{ed}{s_{\ell}(x)}\right)}{n}\right].

If, in addition, Assumption
5
holds, then with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in\mathcal{C}_{2}\left(S_{\ell}(x),\vartheta_{\ell}\right)\cap B_{d}(0,1)}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\pi}_{i}\left[X_{i}^{T}v\right]^{2}\right|
